# Полный контекст проекта: Коми кыв

Дата формирования: 2026-08-19 15:52:22

Этот файл создан автоматически скриптом `scripts/export_full_context.py`.
Он содержит полную выгрузку данных сервиса, документации, критериев,
кода, конфигурации и состояния инфраструктуры.

Важно: значения секретов из .env не выгружаются.
Перед передачей файла третьим лицам проверь содержимое вручную.

## 4. Переменные окружения

Значения секретов не выводятся.

### Файл .env.example


````text
POSTGRES_USER=komi_user
POSTGRES_PASSWORD=
POSTGRES_DB=komi_db

JWT_SECRET=

ADMIN_EMAIL=
ADMIN_PASSWORD=
````


### Переменные из .env (значения секретов скрыты)


````text
POSTGRES_USER=komi_user
POSTGRES_PASSWORD=***HIDDEN***
POSTGRES_DB=komi_db
JWT_SECRET=***HIDDEN***
ADMIN_EMAIL=admin@komikyv.ru
ADMIN_PASSWORD=***HIDDEN***
````

### Compose file


````text
services:
  postgres:
    image: postgres:16
    restart: unless-stopped
    environment:
      POSTGRES_USER: ${POSTGRES_USER:-komi_user}
      POSTGRES_PASSWORD: ${POSTGRES_PASSWORD:-komi_password}
      POSTGRES_DB: ${POSTGRES_DB:-komi_db}
    volumes:
      - postgres_data:/var/lib/postgresql/data
    healthcheck:
      test: ["CMD-SHELL", "pg_isready -U $${POSTGRES_USER} -d $${POSTGRES_DB}"]
      interval: 5s
      timeout: 5s
      retries: 10

  backend:
    build: ./backend
    restart: unless-stopped
    environment:
      DATABASE_URL: postgresql+psycopg2://${POSTGRES_USER:-komi_user}:${POSTGRES_PASSWORD:-komi_password}@postgres:5432/${POSTGRES_DB:-komi_db}
      JWT_SECRET: ${JWT_SECRET:-change_me_to_long_random_string}
      ADMIN_EMAIL: ${ADMIN_EMAIL:-admin@komikyv.ru}
      ADMIN_PASSWORD: ${ADMIN_PASSWORD:-Admin123!}
    depends_on:
      postgres:
        condition: service_healthy
    command: uvicorn app.main:app --host 0.0.0.0 --port 8000

  frontend:
    build: ./frontend
    restart: unless-stopped
    ports:
      - "80:80"
    depends_on:
      - backend

volumes:
  postgres_data:
````

### Файл: README.md


````text
# Коми кыв

Интерактивная веб-платформа для изучения коми языка с интеграцией нейросетевых моделей синтеза речи и диалоговых тренажёров.

## О проекте

Платформа предназначена для сохранения и популяризации коми языка через современные образовательные технологии. Система предоставляет пользователям возможность тренировать произношение и вести диалоги с искусственным интеллектом.

## Возможности

- Интерактивные уроки с теорией и упражнениями
- Диалоговые тренажёры с нейросетью
- Анализ и оценка произношения (ASR/TTS)
- Система геймификации и достижений
- Конструктор учебных модулей для преподавателей
- Модерация контента администратором

## Технологический стек

- **Backend**: FastAPI, SQLAlchemy, PostgreSQL 16
- **Frontend**: React 18, TypeScript, Vite
- **ML**: Celery, Redis (планируется интеграция TTS/ASR/NLP)
- **Инфраструктура**: Docker, Docker Compose, Nginx

## Установка и запуск

### Требования

- Docker и Docker Compose
- Git

### Быстрый старт

```bash
# Клонируем репозиторий
git clone https://github.com/LordOfChaos0/komi-kyv.git
cd komi-kyv

# Копируем .env.example в .env
cp .env.example .env

# Запускаем сервисы
docker compose up -d

# Применяем схему базы данных
./scripts/apply_step1.sh
````


### Файл: backend/Dockerfile


````text
FROM python:3.11-slim

WORKDIR /app

COPY requirements.txt .

RUN pip install --no-cache-dir -r requirements.txt

COPY . .

CMD ["uvicorn", "app.main:app", "--host", "0.0.0.0", "--port", "8000"]
````

````text
from jinja2 import ChoiceLoader, FileSystemLoader
from sqladmin import Admin, ModelView
from sqladmin.authentication import AuthenticationBackend
from sqlalchemy import func, select
from starlette.requests import Request

from . import security
from .database import SessionLocal, engine
from .models import (
    Achievement,
    AppUser,
    AuditLog,
    AuthLog,
    Category,
    DialogScenario,
    DialogSession,
    Exercise,
    Lesson,
    LessonProgress,
    MediaFile,
    ModerationLog,
    Module,
    StudentProfile,
    User,
    UserAchievement,
    UserRole,
    Vocabulary,
)


class AdminAuth(AuthenticationBackend):
    async def login(self, request: Request) -> bool:
        form = await request.form()
        email = str(form.get("username", "")).strip().lower()
        password = str(form.get("password", ""))

        if not email or not password:
            return False

        db = SessionLocal()
        try:
            user = db.scalar(
                select(User).where(func.lower(User.email) == email)
            )
            if (
                not user
                or not user.is_active
                or user.deleted_at
                or user.role != UserRole.admin
                or not security.verify_password(password, user.password_hash)
            ):
                return False

            request.session.update({"admin_user_id": user.id})
            return True
        finally:
            db.close()

    async def logout(self, request: Request) -> bool:
        request.session.clear()
        return True

    async def authenticate(self, request: Request) -> bool:
        admin_user_id = request.session.get("admin_user_id")
        if not admin_user_id:
            return False

        db = SessionLocal()
        try:
            user = db.get(User, admin_user_id)
            return bool(
                user
                and user.is_active
                and not user.deleted_at
                and user.role == UserRole.admin
            )
        finally:
            db.close()


def setup_admin(app):
    admin = Admin(
````

### Файл: backend/app/database.py


````text
import os
from datetime import datetime, timezone

from sqlalchemy import create_engine, event
from sqlalchemy.orm import DeclarativeBase, sessionmaker


DATABASE_URL = os.getenv("DATABASE_URL")

if not DATABASE_URL:
    raise RuntimeError("Переменная окружения DATABASE_URL не задана")


engine = create_engine(
    DATABASE_URL,
    pool_pre_ping=True
)

SessionLocal = sessionmaker(
    bind=engine,
    autocommit=False,
    autoflush=False
)


class Base(DeclarativeBase):
    pass


@event.listens_for(Base, "before_insert", propagate=True)
def set_created_at_on_insert(mapper, connection, target):
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)

    if hasattr(target, "created_at") and target.created_at is None:
        target.created_at = now

    if hasattr(target, "updated_at") and target.updated_at is None:
        target.updated_at = now


@event.listens_for(Base, "before_update", propagate=True)
def set_updated_at_on_update(mapper, connection, target):
    if hasattr(target, "updated_at"):
        target.updated_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)


def get_db():
    db = SessionLocal()
    try:
        yield db
    finally:
        db.close()
````

````text
import os
from datetime import datetime

from fastapi import Depends, FastAPI, HTTPException, Query, status
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
from fastapi.security import HTTPAuthorizationCredentials, HTTPBearer
from jose import JWTError, jwt
from sqlalchemy import func, or_, select, text
from sqlalchemy.exc import IntegrityError
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from . import schemas, security
from . import progress as progress_service
from .admin import setup_admin
from .database import Base, SessionLocal, engine, get_db

from .models import (
    Achievement,
    Category,
    Exercise,
    ExerciseType,
    Lesson,
    LessonProgress,
    Module,
    ModuleCategory,
    ModuleStatus,
    StudentProfile,
    User,
    UserAchievement,
    UserRole,
    Vocabulary,
    ModerationAction,
    ModerationLog
)

bearer_scheme = HTTPBearer(auto_error=False)

app = FastAPI(
    title="Коми кыв API",
    description="MVP backend для интерактивной веб-платформы изучения коми языка",
    version="0.1.0"
)

app.add_middleware(
    CORSMiddleware,
    allow_origins=["*"],
    allow_credentials=True,
    allow_methods=["*"],
    allow_headers=["*"],
)

setup_admin(app)

@app.on_event("startup")
def startup() -> None:
    create_initial_admin()
    create_initial_achievements()

def create_initial_achievements() -> None:
    db = SessionLocal()

    try:
        initial_achievements = [
            {
                "title": "Первый урок",
                "description": "Пройди свой первый урок",
                "criteria_jsonb": {
                    "type": "completed_lessons",
                    "value": 1
                },
                "xp_reward": 50
            },
            {
                "title": "Пять уроков",
                "description": "Пройди пять уроков",
                "criteria_jsonb": {
                    "type": "completed_lessons",
                    "value": 5
                },
                "xp_reward": 150
            },
            {
                "title": "Десять уроков",
                "description": "Пройди десять уроков",
                "criteria_jsonb": {
                    "type": "completed_lessons",
                    "value": 10
                },
                "xp_reward": 300
            }
        ]

        for data in initial_achievements:
            existing = db.scalar(
                select(Achievement).where(
                    Achievement.title == data["title"]
                )
            )

            if not existing:
                db.add(Achievement(**data))

        db.commit()
    finally:
        db.close()

def create_initial_admin() -> None:
    admin_email = os.getenv("ADMIN_EMAIL")
    admin_password = os.getenv("ADMIN_PASSWORD")

    if not admin_email or not admin_password:
        return

    db = SessionLocal()

    try:
        admin = db.scalar(
            select(User).where(User.email == admin_email)
        )

        if not admin:
            admin = User(
                email=admin_email,
                password_hash=security.hash_password(admin_password),
                full_name="Администратор",
                role=UserRole.admin,
                is_active=True
            )

            db.add(admin)
            db.commit()
    finally:
        db.close()


def get_current_user(
    credentials: HTTPAuthorizationCredentials | None = Depends(bearer_scheme),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db)
) -> User:
    if credentials is None:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
            detail="Не авторизован",
            headers={"WWW-Authenticate": "Bearer"}
        )

    token = credentials.credentials

    try:
        payload = jwt.decode(
            token,
            security.JWT_SECRET,
            algorithms=[security.JWT_ALGORITHM]
        )

        user_id = int(payload.get("sub"))

    except (JWTError, ValueError):
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
            detail="Некорректный токен",
            headers={"WWW-Authenticate": "Bearer"}
        )

    user = db.get(User, user_id)

    if not user or not user.is_active or user.deleted_at:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
            detail="Пользователь не найден или заблокирован"
        )

    return user


def require_roles(*roles: UserRole):
    def dependency(current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user)) -> User:
        if current_user.role not in roles:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
                detail="Недостаточно прав"
            )

        return current_user

    return dependency


def get_user_or_404(user_id: int, db: Session) -> User:
    user = db.scalar(
        select(User).where(
            User.id == user_id,
            User.deleted_at.is_(None)
        )
    )

    if not user:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
            detail="Пользователь не найден"
        )

    return user


def get_module_or_404(module_id: int, db: Session) -> Module:
    module = db.scalar(
        select(Module).where(
            Module.id == module_id,
            Module.deleted_at.is_(None)
        )
    )

    if not module:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
            detail="Учебный модуль не найден"
        )

    return module


def can_view_module(module: Module, user: User) -> bool:
    if user.role == UserRole.admin:
        return True

    if module.status == ModuleStatus.published:
        return True

    if user.role == UserRole.teacher and module.author_id == user.id:
        return True

    return False


def can_edit_module(module: Module, user: User) -> bool:
    if module.status == ModuleStatus.archived:
        return False

    if user.role == UserRole.admin:
        return True

    if user.role == UserRole.teacher and module.author_id == user.id:
        return module.status in [
            ModuleStatus.draft,
            ModuleStatus.rejected
        ]

    return False


def can_delete_module(module: Module, user: User) -> bool:
    if user.role == UserRole.admin:
        return True

    if user.role == UserRole.teacher and module.author_id == user.id:
        return module.status in [
            ModuleStatus.draft,
            ModuleStatus.rejected
        ]

    return False


def set_module_status(
    module: Module,
    new_status: ModuleStatus,
    allowed_from: list[ModuleStatus]
) -> None:
    if module.status not in allowed_from:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT,
            detail="Недопустимый переход статуса"
        )

    module.status = new_status

@app.post(
    "/api/auth/register",
    response_model=schemas.UserOut,
    status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED
)
def register(
    payload: schemas.UserCreate,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db)
):
    existing_user = db.scalar(
        select(User).where(User.email == payload.email)
    )

    if existing_user:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT,
            detail="Пользователь с таким email уже существует"
        )

    user = User(
        email=payload.email,
        password_hash=security.hash_password(payload.password),
        full_name=payload.full_name,
        role=UserRole.student,
        is_active=True,
        pd_consent_at=datetime.utcnow()
    )

    db.add(user)
    db.flush()

    profile = StudentProfile(
        user_id=user.id,
        xp=0,
        current_streak=0,
        settings_jsonb={}
    )

    db.add(profile)
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(user)

    return user

@app.post(
    "/api/auth/login",
    response_model=schemas.Token
)
def login(
    payload: schemas.Login,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db)
):
    user = db.scalar(
        select(User).where(User.email == payload.email)
    )

    if (
        not user
        or not user.is_active
        or user.deleted_at
        or not security.verify_password(payload.password, user.password_hash)
    ):
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
            detail="Неверный email или пароль"
        )

    access_token = security.create_access_token(user.id)

    return schemas.Token(access_token=access_token)


@app.get(
    "/api/auth/me",
    response_model=schemas.UserOut
)
def me(current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user)):
    return current_user


@app.get(
    "/api/users",
    response_model=list[schemas.UserOut]
)
def list_users(
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    current_user: User = Depends(require_roles(UserRole.admin))
):
    users = db.scalars(
        select(User)
        .where(User.deleted_at.is_(None))
        .order_by(User.created_at.desc())
    ).all()

    return users


@app.post(
    "/api/users/{user_id}/block",
    response_model=schemas.UserOut
)
def block_user(
    user_id: int,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    current_user: User = Depends(require_roles(UserRole.admin))
):
    if user_id == current_user.id:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT,
            detail="Нельзя заблокировать самого себя"
        )

    user = get_user_or_404(user_id, db)
    user.is_active = False

    db.commit()
    db.refresh(user)

    return user


@app.post(
    "/api/users/{user_id}/unblock",
    response_model=schemas.UserOut
)
def unblock_user(
    user_id: int,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    current_user: User = Depends(require_roles(UserRole.admin))
):
    user = get_user_or_404(user_id, db)
    user.is_active = True

    db.commit()
    db.refresh(user)

    return user


@app.get(
    "/api/modules",
    response_model=schemas.ModulePage
)
def list_modules(
    search: str | None = None,
    status_filter: ModuleStatus | None = Query(default=None, alias="status"),
    level: str | None = None,
    author_id: int | None = None,
    page: int = 1,
    page_size: int = 10,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user)
):
    if page < 1:
        page = 1

    if page_size < 1:
        page_size = 10

    if page_size > 100:
        page_size = 100

    query = select(Module).where(Module.deleted_at.is_(None))

    if current_user.role == UserRole.student:
        query = query.where(Module.status == ModuleStatus.published)

    if current_user.role == UserRole.teacher:
        query = query.where(
            or_(
                Module.author_id == current_user.id,
                Module.status == ModuleStatus.published
            )
        )

    if search:
        query = query.where(Module.title.ilike(f"%{search}%"))

    if status_filter:
        query = query.where(Module.status == status_filter)

    if level:
        query = query.where(Module.level == level)

    if author_id:
        query = query.where(Module.author_id == author_id)

    total = db.scalar(
        select(func.count(Module.id)).select_from(query.subquery())
    )

    modules = db.scalars(
        query
        .order_by(Module.created_at.desc())
        .offset((page - 1) * page_size)
        .limit(page_size)
    ).all()

    return schemas.ModulePage(
        items=modules,
        total=total or 0,
        page=page,
        page_size=page_size
    )


@app.post(
    "/api/modules",
    response_model=schemas.ModuleOut,
    status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED
)
def create_module(
    payload: schemas.ModuleCreate,

    current_user: User = Depends(
        require_roles(UserRole.teacher, UserRole.admin)
    )
):
    module = Module(
        title=payload.title,
        description=payload.description,
        level=payload.level,
        status=ModuleStatus.draft,
        author_id=current_user.id
    )

    db.add(module)
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(module)

    return module

@app.get(
    "/api/modules/{module_id}",
    response_model=schemas.ModuleOut
)
def get_module(
    module_id: int,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user)
):
    module = get_module_or_404(module_id, db)

    if not can_view_module(module, current_user):
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
            detail="Недостаточно прав для просмотра модуля"
        )

    return module


@app.patch(
    "/api/modules/{module_id}",
    response_model=schemas.ModuleOut
)
def update_module(
    module_id: int,
    payload: schemas.ModuleUpdate,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user)
):
    module = get_module_or_404(module_id, db)

    if not can_edit_module(module, current_user):
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
            detail="Недостаточно прав для редактирования модуля"
        )

    update_data = payload.model_dump(exclude_unset=True)

    for field, value in update_data.items():
        setattr(module, field, value)

    db.commit()
    db.refresh(module)

    return module


@app.delete(
    "/api/modules/{module_id}",
    status_code=status.HTTP_204_NO_CONTENT
)
def delete_module(
    module_id: int,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user)
):
    module = get_module_or_404(module_id, db)

    if not can_delete_module(module, current_user):
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
            detail="Недостаточно прав для удаления модуля"
        )

    module.deleted_at = datetime.utcnow()

    db.commit()

    return None


@app.post(
    "/api/modules/{module_id}/submit",
    response_model=schemas.ModuleOut
)
def submit_module(
    module_id: int,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user)
):
    module = get_module_or_404(module_id, db)

    if current_user.role != UserRole.admin and module.author_id != current_user.id:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
            detail="Недостаточно прав для отправки модуля на модерацию"
        )


    set_module_status(
        module=module,
        new_status=ModuleStatus.moderation,
        allowed_from=[ModuleStatus.draft, ModuleStatus.rejected]
    )

    db.commit()
    db.refresh(module)

    return module

):
    module = get_module_or_404(module_id, db)

    set_module_status(

        new_status=ModuleStatus.approved,
        allowed_from=[ModuleStatus.moderation]
    )

    db.commit()
    db.refresh(module)

    return module


@app.post(

    db.commit()

@app.post(
    "/api/modules/{module_id}/publish",
    response_model=schemas.ModuleOut
)
def publish_module(
    module_id: int,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user)
):
    module = get_module_or_404(module_id, db)

    # Публиковать может админ или преподаватель-автор
    if current_user.role != UserRole.admin and module.author_id != current_user.id:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
            detail="Недостаточно прав для публикации модуля"
        )

    set_module_status(
        module=module,
        new_status=ModuleStatus.published,
        allowed_from=[ModuleStatus.approved]
    )

    module.published_at = datetime.utcnow()

    db.commit()
    db.refresh(module)
    return module

):
    module = get_module_or_404(module_id, db)

    set_module_status(
        module=module,
        new_status=ModuleStatus.archived,
        allowed_from=[ModuleStatus.published]
    )

    db.commit()
    db.refresh(module)

    return module

@app.get(
    "/api/modules/{module_id}/lessons",
    response_model=schemas.LessonPage
)
def list_lessons(
    module_id: int,
    page: int = 1,
    page_size: int = 20,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user)
):
    if page < 1:
        page = 1

    if page_size < 1:
        page_size = 20

    if page_size > 100:
        page_size = 100

    module = get_module_or_404(module_id, db)

    if not can_view_module(module, current_user):
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
            detail="Недостаточно прав для просмотра уроков модуля"
        )

    query = select(Lesson).where(
        Lesson.module_id == module_id,
        Lesson.deleted_at.is_(None)
    )

    total = db.scalar(
        select(func.count(Lesson.id)).select_from(query.subquery())
    )

    lessons = db.scalars(
        query
        .order_by(Lesson.order_index.asc(), Lesson.id.asc())
        .offset((page - 1) * page_size)
        .limit(page_size)
    ).all()

    return schemas.LessonPage(
        items=lessons,
        total=total or 0,
        page=page,
        page_size=page_size
    )


@app.post(
    "/api/modules/{module_id}/lessons",
    response_model=schemas.LessonOut,
    status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED
)
def create_lesson(
    module_id: int,
    payload: schemas.LessonCreate,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    current_user: User = Depends(
        require_roles(UserRole.teacher, UserRole.admin)
    )
):
    module = get_module_or_404(module_id, db)

    if not can_edit_module(module, current_user):
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
            detail="Недостаточно прав для добавления урока в модуль"
        )

    lesson = Lesson(
        module_id=module.id,
        title=payload.title,
        order_index=payload.order_index,
        theory_content=payload.theory_content,
        passing_score=payload.passing_score,
        max_attempts=payload.max_attempts
    )

    db.add(lesson)

    try:
        db.commit()
    except IntegrityError:
        db.rollback()
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT,
            detail="Урок с таким order_index уже существует в этом модуле"
        )

    db.refresh(lesson)
    return lesson

@app.get(
    "/api/lessons/{lesson_id}/exercises",
    response_model=schemas.ExercisePage
)
def list_exercises(
    lesson_id: int,
    exercise_type: ExerciseType | None = Query(default=None, alias="type"),
    page: int = 1,
    page_size: int = 20,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user)
):
    if page < 1:
        page = 1

    if page_size < 1:
        page_size = 20

    if page_size > 100:
        page_size = 100

    lesson = get_lesson_or_404(lesson_id, db)
    module = get_module_or_404(lesson.module_id, db)

    if not can_view_module(module, current_user):
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
            detail="Недостаточно прав для просмотра упражнений урока"
        )

    query = select(Exercise).where(
        Exercise.lesson_id == lesson_id,
        Exercise.deleted_at.is_(None)
    )

    if exercise_type:
        query = query.where(Exercise.type == exercise_type)

    total = db.scalar(
        select(func.count(Exercise.id)).select_from(query.subquery())
    )

    exercises = db.scalars(
        query
        .order_by(Exercise.id.asc())
        .offset((page - 1) * page_size)
        .limit(page_size)
    ).all()

    return schemas.ExercisePage(
        items=exercises,
        total=total or 0,
        page=page,
        page_size=page_size
    )

@app.post(
    "/api/exercises/{exercise_id}/check",
    response_model=schemas.ExerciseCheckResult
)
def check_exercise(
    exercise_id: int,
    payload: schemas.ExerciseCheck,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user)
):
    exercise = get_exercise_or_404(exercise_id, db)
    lesson = get_lesson_or_404(exercise.lesson_id, db)
    module = get_module_or_404(lesson.module_id, db)

    if not can_view_module(module, current_user):
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
            detail="Недостаточно прав для проверки упражнения"
        )

    is_correct = False

    if exercise.type == ExerciseType.matching:
        # Для matching сравниваем JSON ответы
        try:
            import json
            user_answer = json.loads(payload.answer)
            correct_answer = json.loads(exercise.correct_answer)

            # correct_answer хранится как список пар [{left, right}]
            # user_answer хранится как {left: right}
            if isinstance(correct_answer, list) and isinstance(user_answer, dict):
                expected = {pair["left"]: pair["right"] for pair in correct_answer}
                is_correct = user_answer == expected
            else:
                is_correct = False
        except (json.JSONDecodeError, KeyError, TypeError):
            is_correct = False
    else:
        # Для остальных типов сравниваем строки
        normalized_answer = payload.answer.strip().lower()
        normalized_correct_answer = exercise.correct_answer.strip().lower()
        is_correct = normalized_answer == normalized_correct_answer

    if is_correct:
        return schemas.ExerciseCheckResult(
            is_correct=True,
            message="Правильный ответ"
        )

    return schemas.ExerciseCheckResult(
        is_correct=False,
        message="Пока неправильно",
        hint=exercise.hint
    )

@app.post(
    "/api/lessons/{lesson_id}/complete",
    response_model=schemas.LessonProgressOut
)

    lesson_id: int,

    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user)
):
    lesson = db.get(Lesson, lesson_id)

    if not lesson:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
            detail="Урок не найден"
        )

    module = db.get(Module, lesson.module_id)

    if not module or not can_view_module(module, current_user):
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
            detail="Недостаточно прав для прохождения урока"
        )

    progress = progress_service.complete_lesson(
        user_id=current_user.id,
        lesson_id=lesson_id,
        score=payload.score,
        db=db
    )

    db.commit()

    return progress

)
def get_my_achievements(
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user)
):
    rows = db.execute(
        select(UserAchievement, Achievement)
        .join(
            Achievement,
            UserAchievement.achievement_id == Achievement.id
        )
        .where(UserAchievement.user_id == current_user.id)
        .order_by(UserAchievement.received_at.desc())
    ).all()

    result = []

    for user_achievement, achievement in rows:
        result.append(
            {
                "achievement": achievement,
                "received_at": user_achievement.received_at
            }
        )

    return result

@app.get(
    "/api/vocabulary",
    response_model=schemas.VocabularyPage
)
def search_vocabulary(
    search: str | None = None,
    lesson_id: int | None = None,
    page: int = 1,
    page_size: int = 20,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user)
):
    if page < 1:
        page = 1

    if page_size < 1:
        page_size = 20

    if page_size > 100:
        page_size = 100

    query = select(Vocabulary).where(
        Vocabulary.deleted_at.is_(None)
    )

    if lesson_id:
        query = query.where(Vocabulary.lesson_id == lesson_id)

    if search:
        search_pattern = f"%{search}%"
        query = query.where(
            or_(
                Vocabulary.word_komi.ilike(search_pattern),
                Vocabulary.translation_ru.ilike(search_pattern)
            )
        )

    total = db.scalar(
        select(func.count(Vocabulary.id)).select_from(query.subquery())
    )

    items = db.scalars(
        query
        .order_by(Vocabulary.word_komi.asc())
        .offset((page - 1) * page_size)
        .limit(page_size)
    ).all()

    return schemas.VocabularyPage(
        items=items,
        total=total or 0,
        page=page,
        page_size=page_size
    )

@app.post(
    "/api/categories",
    response_model=schemas.CategoryOut,
    status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED
)
def create_category(
    payload: schemas.CategoryCreate,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    current_user: User = Depends(require_roles(UserRole.admin))
):
    existing = db.scalar(
        select(Category).where(
            or_(
                Category.name == payload.name,
                Category.slug == payload.slug
            )
        )
    )

    if existing:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT,
            detail="Категория с таким названием или slug уже существует"
        )

    category = Category(
        name=payload.name,
        slug=payload.slug,
        description=payload.description
    )

    db.add(category)
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(category)

    return category

            detail="Недостаточно прав для просмотра категорий модуля"
        )

    categories = db.scalars(
        select(Category)
        .join(

        )
        .where(ModuleCategory.module_id == module_id)
        .order_by(Category.name.asc())
    ).all()

    return categories


@app.post(
    "/api/modules/{module_id}/categories/{category_id}",
    status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED
)
def add_category_to_module(
    module_id: int,
    category_id: int,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    current_user: User = Depends(
        require_roles(UserRole.teacher, UserRole.admin)
    )
):
    module = get_module_or_404(module_id, db)

    if not can_edit_module(module, current_user):
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
            detail="Недостаточно прав для добавления категории к модулю"
        )

    category = get_category_or_404(category_id, db)

    existing = db.scalar(
        select(ModuleCategory).where(
            ModuleCategory.module_id == module_id,
            ModuleCategory.category_id == category_id
        )
    )

    if existing:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT,
            detail="Категория уже добавлена к модулю"
        )

    module_category = ModuleCategory(
        module_id=module_id,
        category_id=category_id
    )

    db.add(module_category)
    db.commit()

    return {
        "message": "Категория добавлена к модулю"
    }

    "/api/modules/{module_id}/categories/{category_id}",
    status_code=status.HTTP_204_NO_CONTENT
)
def remove_category_from_module(
    module_id: int,
    category_id: int,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    current_user: User = Depends(
        require_roles(UserRole.teacher, UserRole.admin)
    )
):

    if not can_edit_module(module, current_user):
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
            detail="Недостаточно прав для удаления категории из модуля"
        )

    module_category = db.scalar(
        select(ModuleCategory).where(
            ModuleCategory.module_id == module_id,
            ModuleCategory.category_id == category_id
        )
    )

    if not module_category:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
            detail="Категория не найдена в модуле"
        )

    db.delete(module_category)
    db.commit()

    return None

@app.post(
    "/api/modules/{module_id}/withdraw",
    response_model=schemas.ModuleOut
)
def withdraw_module(
    module_id: int,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user)
):
    module = get_module_or_404(module_id, db)

    # Отозвать может только автор модуля или администратор
    if current_user.role != UserRole.admin and module.author_id != current_user.id:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
            detail="Недостаточно прав для отзыва модуля с модерации"
        )

    set_module_status(
        module=module,
        new_status=ModuleStatus.draft,
        allowed_from=[ModuleStatus.moderation]
    )

    db.commit()
    db.refresh(module)
    return module

@app.post(
    "/api/modules/{module_id}/unpublish",
    response_model=schemas.ModuleOut
)
def unpublish_module(
    module_id: int,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user)
):
    """Снять модуль с публикации и вернуть в черновики."""
    module = get_module_or_404(module_id, db)

    # Снять с публикации может только администратор или автор модуля
    if current_user.role != UserRole.admin and module.author_id != current_user.id:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
            detail="Недостаточно прав для снятия модуля с публикации"
        )

    set_module_status(
        module=module,
        new_status=ModuleStatus.draft,
        allowed_from=[ModuleStatus.published]
    )

    # Логирование действия модерации
    moderation_log = ModerationLog(
        module_id=module.id,
        admin_id=current_user.id,
        action=ModerationAction.update,
        comment="Модуль снят с публикации и возвращён в черновики"
    )
    db.add(moderation_log)

    db.commit()
    db.refresh(module)
    return module

@app.get(
    "/api/admin/dashboard",
    response_model=schemas.AdminDashboardResponse
)
def get_admin_dashboard(
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    current_user: User = Depends(require_roles(UserRole.admin))
):
    """Дашборд администратора со статистикой."""
    from datetime import timedelta, timezone

    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    thirty_days_ago = now - timedelta(days=30)

    # Пользователи
    total_users = db.scalar(
        select(func.count(User.id)).where(User.deleted_at.is_(None))
    ) or 0

    active_users = db.scalar(
        select(func.count(User.id)).where(
            User.deleted_at.is_(None),
            User.is_active.is_(True)
        )
    ) or 0

    students_count = db.scalar(
        select(func.count(User.id)).where(
            User.deleted_at.is_(None),
            User.role == UserRole.student
        )
    ) or 0

    teachers_count = db.scalar(
        select(func.count(User.id)).where(
            User.deleted_at.is_(None),
            User.role == UserRole.teacher
        )
    ) or 0

    # Модули
    total_modules = db.scalar(
        select(func.count(Module.id)).where(Module.deleted_at.is_(None))
    ) or 0

    modules_in_moderation = db.scalar(
        select(func.count(Module.id)).where(
            Module.deleted_at.is_(None),
            Module.status == ModuleStatus.moderation
        )
    ) or 0

    published_modules = db.scalar(
        select(func.count(Module.id)).where(
            Module.deleted_at.is_(None),
            Module.status == ModuleStatus.published
        )
    ) or 0

    # Модули по статусам
    modules_by_status_rows = db.execute(
        select(Module.status, func.count(Module.id))
        .where(Module.deleted_at.is_(None))
        .group_by(Module.status)
        .order_by(func.count(Module.id).desc())
    ).all()
    modules_by_status = [
        {"status": row[0].value, "count": row[1]}
        for row in modules_by_status_rows
    ]

    # Пройденные уроки
    completed_lessons = db.scalar(
        select(func.count(LessonProgress.id)).where(
            LessonProgress.is_completed.is_(True)
        )
    ) or 0

    # Регистрации по дням (последние 30 дней)
    registrations_rows = db.execute(
        select(
            func.date(User.created_at).label("day"),
            func.count(User.id)
        )
        .where(
            User.created_at >= thirty_days_ago,
            User.deleted_at.is_(None)
        )
        .group_by(func.date(User.created_at))
        .order_by(func.date(User.created_at))
    ).all()

    registrations_map = {str(row[0]): row[1] for row in registrations_rows}
    registrations_dates = []
    registrations_counts = []
    for i in range(29, -1, -1):
        day = (now - timedelta(days=i)).date().isoformat()
        registrations_dates.append(day)
        registrations_counts.append(registrations_map.get(day, 0))

    # Прохождения уроков по дням (последние 30 дней)
    lesson_rows = db.execute(
        select(
            func.date(LessonProgress.created_at).label("day"),
            func.count(LessonProgress.id)
        )
        .where(
            LessonProgress.created_at >= thirty_days_ago,
            LessonProgress.is_completed.is_(True)
        )
        .group_by(func.date(LessonProgress.created_at))
        .order_by(func.date(LessonProgress.created_at))
    ).all()

    lesson_map = {str(row[0]): row[1] for row in lesson_rows}
    lesson_dates = []
    lesson_counts = []
    for i in range(29, -1, -1):
        day = (now - timedelta(days=i)).date().isoformat()
        lesson_dates.append(day)
        lesson_counts.append(lesson_map.get(day, 0))

    return schemas.AdminDashboardResponse(
        total_users=total_users,
        active_users=active_users,
        students_count=students_count,
        teachers_count=teachers_count,
        total_modules=total_modules,
        modules_in_moderation=modules_in_moderation,
        published_modules=published_modules,
        modules_by_status=modules_by_status,
        completed_lessons=completed_lessons,
        registrations_dates=registrations_dates,
        registrations_counts=registrations_counts,
        lesson_dates=lesson_dates,
        lesson_counts=lesson_counts,
    )
````

````text
from datetime import datetime

from sqlalchemy import func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .models import (
    Achievement,
    Lesson,
    LessonProgress,
    StudentProfile,
    UserAchievement,
    UserRole,
)


XP_PER_SCORE_POINT = 1


def get_or_create_student_profile(user_id: int, db: Session) -> StudentProfile:
    profile = db.get(StudentProfile, user_id)

    if profile is None:
        profile = StudentProfile(
            user_id=user_id,
            xp=0,
            current_streak=0,
            settings_jsonb={}
        )
        db.add(profile)
        db.flush()

    return profile


def complete_lesson(
    user_id: int,
    lesson_id: int,
    score: int,
    db: Session
) -> LessonProgress:
    lesson = db.get(Lesson, lesson_id)

    if not lesson:
        raise ValueError("Урок не найден")

    last_attempt = db.scalar(
        select(func.max(LessonProgress.attempt_number)).where(
            LessonProgress.user_id == user_id,
            LessonProgress.lesson_id == lesson_id
        )
    )

    attempt_number = (last_attempt or 0) + 1

    is_completed = score >= lesson.passing_score

    progress = LessonProgress(
        user_id=user_id,
        lesson_id=lesson_id,
        score=score,
        is_completed=is_completed,
        attempt_number=attempt_number
    )

    db.add(progress)
    db.flush()

    if is_completed:
        profile = get_or_create_student_profile(user_id, db)
        xp_gain = score * XP_PER_SCORE_POINT
        profile.xp += xp_gain

    check_achievements(user_id, db)

    return progress


def check_achievements(user_id: int, db: Session) -> list[Achievement]:
    new_achievements: list[Achievement] = []

    completed_count = db.scalar(
        select(func.count(func.distinct(LessonProgress.lesson_id))).where(
            LessonProgress.user_id == user_id,
            LessonProgress.is_completed.is_(True)
        )
    ) or 0

    achievements = db.scalars(
        select(Achievement)
    ).all()

    already_received_ids = set(
        db.scalars(
            select(UserAchievement.achievement_id).where(
                UserAchievement.user_id == user_id
            )
        ).all()
    )

    for achievement in achievements:
        if achievement.id in already_received_ids:
            continue

        criteria = achievement.criteria_jsonb or {}
        condition_type = criteria.get("type")

        if condition_type == "completed_lessons":
            required = int(criteria.get("value", 0))
            if completed_count >= required:
                db.add(
                    UserAchievement(
                        user_id=user_id,
                        achievement_id=achievement.id,
                        received_at=datetime.utcnow()
                    )
                )

                profile = get_or_create_student_profile(user_id, db)
                profile.xp += achievement.xp_reward

                new_achievements.append(achievement)

    return new_achievements


def get_progress_summary(user_id: int, db: Session) -> dict:
    profile = get_or_create_student_profile(user_id, db)

    completed_lessons = db.scalar(
        select(func.count(func.distinct(LessonProgress.lesson_id))).where(
            LessonProgress.user_id == user_id,
            LessonProgress.is_completed.is_(True)
        )
    ) or 0

    total_achievements = db.scalar(
        select(func.count(UserAchievement.achievement_id)).where(
            UserAchievement.user_id == user_id
        )
    ) or 0

    return {
        "xp": profile.xp,
        "level": profile.level.value,
        "current_streak": profile.current_streak,
        "completed_lessons": completed_lessons,
        "total_achievements": total_achievements,
    }
````

### Файл: backend/app/security.py


````text
import os
from datetime import datetime, timedelta

from jose import jwt
from passlib.context import CryptContext


pwd_context = CryptContext(
    schemes=["bcrypt"],
    deprecated="auto"
)

JWT_SECRET = os.getenv("JWT_SECRET", "change_me_to_long_random_string")
JWT_ALGORITHM = "HS256"
ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES = int(
    os.getenv("ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES", "120")
)


def hash_password(password: str) -> str:
    return pwd_context.hash(password)


def verify_password(plain_password: str, hashed_password: str) -> bool:
    return pwd_context.verify(plain_password, hashed_password)


def create_access_token(user_id: int) -> str:
    expire = datetime.utcnow() + timedelta(
        minutes=ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES
    )

    payload = {
        "sub": str(user_id),
        "exp": expire
    }

    return jwt.encode(
        payload,
        JWT_SECRET,
        algorithm=JWT_ALGORITHM
    )
````

````text
                plugins: { legend: { display: false } },
                scales: {
                    y: { beginAtZero: true, ticks: { stepSize: 1 } }
                }
            }
        });
    </script>
</body>
</html>
````

### Файл: deploy.sh


````text
#!/bin/bash
set -e

echo "========================================="
echo " Установка зависимостей и деплой Коми кыв"
echo " ОС: Debian 13 (Trixie)"
echo "========================================="

# 1. Обновление системы и установка базовых утилит
echo "[1/6] Обновление системы и установка базовых пакетов..."
sudo apt update && sudo apt upgrade -y
sudo apt install -y curl gnupg lsb-release ca-certificates git python3-pip python3-venv

# 2. Установка Docker CE (с поддержкой Debian Trixie через bookworm fallback)
echo "[2/6] Установка Docker Engine..."
if ! command -v docker &> /dev/null; then
    # Добавляем GPG ключ Docker
    sudo install -m 0755 -d /etc/apt/keyrings
    curl -fsSL https://download.docker.com/linux/debian/gpg | sudo gpg --dearmor -o /etc/apt/keyrings/docker.gpg
    sudo chmod a+r /etc/apt/keyrings/docker.gpg

    # Добавляем репозиторий. Для Trixie используем bookworm, так как trixie может быть недоступен
    CODENAME=$(. /etc/os-release && echo "$VERSION_CODENAME")
    if [ "$CODENAME" = "trixie" ]; then
        FALLBACK_CODENAME="bookworm"
        echo "⚠️  Debian Trixie обнаружен. Используем репозиторий bookworm для Docker."
    else
        FALLBACK_CODENAME="$CODENAME"
    fi

    echo \
      "deb [arch=$(dpkg --print-architecture) signed-by=/etc/apt/keyrings/docker.gpg] https://download.docker.com/linux/debian \
      $FALLBACK_CODENAME stable" | sudo tee /etc/apt/sources.list.d/docker.list > /dev/null

    sudo apt update
    sudo apt install -y docker-ce docker-ce-cli containerd.io docker-buildx-plugin docker-compose-plugin
    
    # Добавляем текущего пользователя в группу docker (чтобы не нужен был sudo)
    sudo usermod -aG docker $USER
    echo "✅ Docker установлен. Требуется перезагрузка или перелогин для применения прав группы docker."
else
    echo "✅ Docker уже установлен."
fi

# 3. Проверка наличия файлов проекта
echo "[3/6] Проверка структуры проекта..."
if [ ! -f "docker-compose.yml" ] || [ ! -f "backend/requirements.txt" ]; then
    echo "❌ Ошибка: Не найдены файлы docker-compose.yml или backend/requirements.txt"
    echo "Убедись, что скрипт запускается из корневой папки проекта 'komi-kyv'"
    exit 1
fi

# 4. Создание .env файла, если его нет
echo "[4/6] Настройка переменных окружения..."
if [ ! -f ".env" ]; then
    if [ -f ".env.example" ]; then
        cp .env.example .env
        echo "✅ Файл .env создан из .env.example"
        echo "⚠️  ОБЯЗАТЕЛЬНО отредактируйте .env и смените пароли перед боевым использованием!"
    else
        echo "❌ Ошибка: Не найден .env.example. Создайте .env вручную."
        exit 1
    fi
fi

# 5. Создание директорий для данных
echo "[5/6] Подготовка томов для данных..."
mkdir -p media backups logs

# 6. Сборка и запуск контейнеров
echo "[6/6] Сборка образов и запуск сервисов..."
sudo docker compose build --no-cache
sudo docker compose up -d

echo "========================================="
echo " ✅ Развёртывание завершено!"
echo "========================================="
echo ""
echo "📌 Полезные команды:"
echo "   Логи:          sudo docker compose logs -f"
echo "   Статус:        sudo docker compose ps"
echo "   Остановка:     sudo docker compose down"
echo "   Перезапуск:    sudo docker compose restart"
echo ""
echo "🌐 Сервис доступен по адресу: http://localhost"
echo "📚 Swagger API:             http://localhost/docs"
echo ""
echo "⏳ База данных инициализируется ~10-15 секунд."
echo "   Если сайт не открывается сразу, подождите и проверьте логи."
````


### Файл: docker-compose.yml


````text
services:
  postgres:
    image: postgres:16
    restart: unless-stopped
    environment:
      POSTGRES_USER: ${POSTGRES_USER:-komi_user}
      POSTGRES_PASSWORD: ${POSTGRES_PASSWORD:-komi_password}
      POSTGRES_DB: ${POSTGRES_DB:-komi_db}
    volumes:
      - postgres_data:/var/lib/postgresql/data
    healthcheck:
      test: ["CMD-SHELL", "pg_isready -U $${POSTGRES_USER} -d $${POSTGRES_DB}"]
      interval: 5s
      timeout: 5s
      retries: 10

  backend:
    build: ./backend
    restart: unless-stopped
    environment:
      DATABASE_URL: postgresql+psycopg2://${POSTGRES_USER:-komi_user}:${POSTGRES_PASSWORD:-komi_password}@postgres:5432/${POSTGRES_DB:-komi_db}
      JWT_SECRET: ${JWT_SECRET:-change_me_to_long_random_string}
      ADMIN_EMAIL: ${ADMIN_EMAIL:-admin@komikyv.ru}
      ADMIN_PASSWORD: ${ADMIN_PASSWORD:-Admin123!}
    depends_on:
      postgres:
        condition: service_healthy
    command: uvicorn app.main:app --host 0.0.0.0 --port 8000

  frontend:
    build: ./frontend
    restart: unless-stopped
    ports:
      - "80:80"
    depends_on:
      - backend

volumes:
  postgres_data:
````

### Файл: frontend/nginx.conf


````text
server {
    listen 80;
    server_name _;
    client_max_body_size 20M;
    root /usr/share/nginx/html;
    index index.html;

    location / {
        try_files $uri /index.html;
    }

    location /api/ {
        proxy_pass http://backend:8000/api/;
        proxy_set_header Host $host;
        proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto $scheme;
        proxy_read_timeout 120s;
        proxy_connect_timeout 120s;
        proxy_send_timeout 120s;
    }

    location /docs {
        proxy_pass http://backend:8000/docs;
        proxy_set_header Host $host;
        proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto $scheme;
    }

    location /openapi.json {
        proxy_pass http://backend:8000/openapi.json;
        proxy_set_header Host $host;
        proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto $scheme;
    }

    # Точное совпадение для /admin (без слеша)
    location = /admin {
        proxy_pass http://backend:8000/admin/;
        proxy_set_header Host $host;
        proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto $scheme;
    }

    # Все пути, начинающиеся с /admin/ (со слешем)
    location /admin/ {
        proxy_pass http://backend:8000/admin/;
        proxy_set_header Host $host;
        proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto $scheme;
    }
}
````

````text
import { useState } from "react";
import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import {
  BrowserRouter,
  Link,
  Navigate,
  Route,
  Routes,
} from "react-router-dom";

import { AuthProvider, useAuth } from "./auth";
import Sidebar from "./components/Sidebar";
import HomePage from "./pages/HomePage";
import LoginPage from "./pages/LoginPage";
import RegisterPage from "./pages/RegisterPage";
import ModulesPage from "./pages/ModulesPage";
import ModuleDetailPage from "./pages/ModuleDetailPage";
import LessonPage from "./pages/LessonPage";
import DashboardPage from "./pages/DashboardPage";
import ProgressPage from "./pages/ProgressPage";
import AchievementsPage from "./pages/AchievementsPage";
import VocabularyPage from "./pages/VocabularyPage";
import CategoriesPage from "./pages/CategoriesPage";
import ProfilePage from "./pages/ProfilePage";
import AdminDashboardPage from "./pages/AdminDashboardPage";

function Protected({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const { user, loading } = useAuth();

  if (loading) {
    return <div className="container">Загрузка...</div>;
  }

  if (!user) {
    return <Navigate to="/login" replace />;
  }

  return <>{children}</>;
}

function Header({ onMenuClick }: { onMenuClick: () => void }) {
  const { user } = useAuth();

  return (
    <header className="header">
      <div className="container header-inner">
        {user ? (
          <button
            type="button"
            className="menu-toggle"
            onClick={onMenuClick}
          >
            ☰
          </button>
        ) : (
          <div className="menu-toggle-placeholder" />
        )}

        <Link to="/" className="brand">
          Коми кыв
        </Link>

        <nav className="nav">
          {!user ? (
            <>
              <Link to="/login">Вход</Link>
              <Link to="/register">Регистрация</Link>
            </>
          ) : (
            <span className="nav-user">
              {user.full_name || user.email}
            </span>
          )}
        </nav>
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}

function AppContent() {
  const [sidebarOpen, setSidebarOpen] = useState(false);
  const { user } = useAuth();

  return (
    <BrowserRouter>
      <Header onMenuClick={() => setSidebarOpen(true)} />

      {user && (
        <Sidebar
          isOpen={sidebarOpen}
          onClose={() => setSidebarOpen(false)}
        />
      )}

      <main className="main">
        <Routes>
          <Route path="/" element={<HomePage />} />
          <Route path="/login" element={<LoginPage />} />
          <Route path="/register" element={<RegisterPage />} />

          <Route
            path="/modules"
            element={
              <Protected>
                <ModulesPage />
              </Protected>
            }
          />

          <Route
            path="/modules/:moduleId"
            element={
              <Protected>
                <ModuleDetailPage />
              </Protected>
            }
          />

          <Route
            path="/lessons/:lessonId"
            element={
              <Protected>
                <LessonPage />
              </Protected>
            }
          />

          <Route
            path="/dashboard"
            element={
              <Protected>
                <DashboardPage />
              </Protected>
            }
          />

          <Route
            path="/progress"
            element={
              <Protected>
                <ProgressPage />
              </Protected>
            }
          />

          <Route
            path="/achievements"
            element={
              <Protected>
                <AchievementsPage />
              </Protected>
            }
          />

          <Route
            path="/vocabulary"
            element={
              <Protected>
                <VocabularyPage />
              </Protected>
            }
          />

          <Route
            path="/categories"
            element={
              <Protected>
                <CategoriesPage />
              </Protected>
            }
          />

          <Route
            path="/profile"
            element={
              <Protected>
                <ProfilePage />
              </Protected>
            }
          />
<Route
  path="/admin-dashboard"
  element={
    <Protected>
      <AdminDashboardPage />
    </Protected>
  }
/>

          <Route
            path="*"
            element={
              <div className="container">
                <div className="card">
                  <h1>404</h1>
                  <p>Страница не найдена.</p>
                </div>
              </div>
            }
          />
        </Routes>
      </main>
    </BrowserRouter>
  );
}

export default function App() {
  return (
    <AuthProvider>
      <AppContent />
    </AuthProvider>
  );
}
````


### Файл: frontend/src/api/client.ts


````text
export class ApiError extends Error {
  status: number;

  constructor(message: string, status: number) {
    super(message);
    this.status = status;
  }
}

async function request<T>(path: string, options: RequestInit = {}): Promise<T> {
  const token = localStorage.getItem("access_token");

  const headers = new Headers(options.headers || {});

  if (token) {
    headers.set("Authorization", `Bearer ${token}`);
  }

  if (options.body && !headers.has("Content-Type")) {
    headers.set("Content-Type", "application/json");
  }

  const response = await fetch(`/api${path}`, {
    ...options,
    headers
  });

  if (!response.ok) {
    let message = `HTTP ${response.status}`;

    try {
      const data = await response.json();

      if (data && typeof data === "object" && "detail" in data) {
        message = String((data as any).detail);
      }
    } catch {
      // Если тело ошибки не JSON, оставляем стандартное сообщение.
    }

    throw new ApiError(message, response.status);
  }

  if (response.status === 204) {
    return undefined as T;
  }

  return response.json() as Promise<T>;
}

export const api = {
  get: <T>(path: string) => request<T>(path, { method: "GET" }),

  post: <T>(path: string, body?: unknown) =>
    request<T>(path, {
      method: "POST",
      body: body === undefined ? undefined : JSON.stringify(body)
    }),

  patch: <T>(path: string, body?: unknown) =>
    request<T>(path, {
      method: "PATCH",
      body: body === undefined ? undefined : JSON.stringify(body)
    }),

  delete: <T>(path: string) => request<T>(path, { method: "DELETE" })
};
````

### Файл: frontend/src/auth.tsx


````text
import { createContext, useContext, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import type { ReactNode } from "react";

import { api } from "./api/client";
import type { TokenResponse, UserOut } from "./types";

interface AuthContextValue {
  user: UserOut | null;
  loading: boolean;
  login: (email: string, password: string) => Promise<void>;
  register: (email: string, password: string, fullName: string) => Promise<void>;
  logout: () => void;
}

const AuthContext = createContext<AuthContextValue | undefined>(undefined);

export function AuthProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [user, setUser] = useState<UserOut | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  const refreshUser = async () => {
    try {
      const me = await api.get<UserOut>("/auth/me");
      setUser(me);
    } catch {
      localStorage.removeItem("access_token");
      setUser(null);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    const token = localStorage.getItem("access_token");

    if (!token) {
      setLoading(false);
      return;
    }

    refreshUser().finally(() => setLoading(false));
  }, []);

  const login = async (email: string, password: string) => {
    const response = await api.post<TokenResponse>("/auth/login", {
      email,
      password
    });

    localStorage.setItem("access_token", response.access_token);
    await refreshUser();
  };

  const register = async (
    email: string,
    password: string,
    fullName: string
  ) => {
    await api.post<UserOut>("/auth/register", {
      email,
      password,
      full_name: fullName || null
    });

    await login(email, password);
  };

  const logout = () => {
    localStorage.removeItem("access_token");
    setUser(null);
  };

  return (
    <AuthContext.Provider
      value={{
        user,
        loading,
        login,
        register,
        logout
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </AuthContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function useAuth() {
  const context = useContext(AuthContext);

  if (!context) {
    throw new Error("useAuth должен использоваться внутри AuthProvider");
  }

  return context;
}
````


### Файл: frontend/src/components/ExerciseForm.tsx


````text
import { useState } from "react";

import { exerciseApi } from "../api/exercises";
import type { ExerciseCreate, ExerciseOut, ExerciseType, MatchingPair } from "../types";

interface ExerciseFormProps {
  lessonId: number;
  exercise?: ExerciseOut;
  onSaved: () => void;
  onCancel: () => void;
}

const EXERCISE_TYPES: Array<{ value: ExerciseType; label: string; description: string }> = [
  {
    value: "translation",
    label: "Перевод",
    description: "Ученик выбирает или вводит перевод слова/фразы",
  },
  {
    value: "matching",
    label: "Сопоставление",
    description: "Ученик соединяет пары: слово на коми — перевод",
  },
  {
    value: "fill_blank",
    label: "Заполни пропуск",
    description: "Ученик вставляет пропущенное слово в предложение",
  },
  {
    value: "audio_recognition",
    label: "Аудио-распознавание",
    description: "Ученик распознаёт слово на слух (пока без аудио)",
  },
];

const EMPTY_PAIR: MatchingPair = { left: "", right: "" };

export default function ExerciseForm({
  lessonId,
  exercise,
  onSaved,
  onCancel,
}: ExerciseFormProps) {
  const [type, setType] = useState<ExerciseType>(exercise?.type || "translation");
  const [question, setQuestion] = useState(exercise?.question || "");
  const [correctAnswer, setCorrectAnswer] = useState(exercise?.correct_answer || "");
  const [hint, setHint] = useState(exercise?.hint || "");
  const [scoreWeight, setScoreWeight] = useState(exercise?.score_weight || 1);
  const [options, setOptions] = useState<string[]>(
    Array.isArray(exercise?.options_jsonb) && typeof exercise.options_jsonb[0] === "string"
      ? (exercise.options_jsonb as string[])
      : ["", "", "", ""]
  );
  const [matchingPairs, setMatchingPairs] = useState<MatchingPair[]>(
    Array.isArray(exercise?.options_jsonb) &&
    exercise.options_jsonb.length > 0 &&
    typeof exercise.options_jsonb[0] === "object"
      ? (exercise.options_jsonb as MatchingPair[])
      : [{ ...EMPTY_PAIR }, { ...EMPTY_PAIR }, { ...EMPTY_PAIR }]
  );

  const [formError, setFormError] = useState("");
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [showPreview, setShowPreview] = useState(false);

  // Определение, нужно ли поле "правильный ответ"
  const needsCorrectAnswer = type !== "matching";

  const handleAddOption = () => {
    setOptions([...options, ""]);
  };

  const handleRemoveOption = (index: number) => {
    if (options.length <= 2) return;
    setOptions(options.filter((_, i) => i !== index));
  };

  const handleOptionChange = (index: number, value: string) => {
    const newOptions = [...options];
    newOptions[index] = value;
    setOptions(newOptions);
  };

  const handleAddPair = () => {
    setMatchingPairs([...matchingPairs, { ...EMPTY_PAIR }]);
  };

  const handleRemovePair = (index: number) => {
    if (matchingPairs.length <= 1) return;
    setMatchingPairs(matchingPairs.filter((_, i) => i !== index));
  };

  const handlePairChange = (index: number, field: "left" | "right", value: string) => {
    const newPairs = [...matchingPairs];
    newPairs[index] = { ...newPairs[index], [field]: value };
    setMatchingPairs(newPairs);
  };

  const validate = (): string | null => {
    if (!question.trim()) {
      return "Вопрос не может быть пустым";
    }

    if (scoreWeight < 1) {
      return "Вес упражнения должен быть не меньше 1";
    }

    if (needsCorrectAnswer && !correctAnswer.trim()) {
      return "Правильный ответ не может быть пустым";
    }

    if (type === "translation" || type === "audio_recognition") {
      const filledOptions = options.filter((o) => o.trim() !== "");
      if (filledOptions.length < 2) {
        return "Добавь минимум два варианта ответа";
      }
      if (!filledOptions.includes(correctAnswer.trim())) {
        return "Правильный ответ должен быть среди вариантов ответа";
      }
    }

    if (type === "matching") {
      const filledPairs = matchingPairs.filter(
        (p) => p.left.trim() !== "" && p.right.trim() !== ""
      );
      if (filledPairs.length < 2) {
        return "Добавь минимум две заполненные пары для сопоставления";
      }
    }

    if (type === "fill_blank" && !question.includes("___")) {
      return "Для упражнения «Заполни пропуск» используй ___ в тексте вопроса для обозначения пропуска";
    }

    return null;
  };

  const handleSubmit = async () => {
    const validationError = validate();
    if (validationError) {
      setFormError(validationError);
      return;
    }

    setSubmitting(true);
    setFormError("");

    try {
      let optionsJsonb: unknown[] = [];
      let finalCorrectAnswer = correctAnswer.trim();

      if (type === "translation" || type === "audio_recognition") {
        optionsJsonb = options.filter((o) => o.trim() !== "");
      }

      if (type === "matching") {
        const filledPairs = matchingPairs.filter(
          (p) => p.left.trim() !== "" && p.right.trim() !== ""
        );
        optionsJsonb = filledPairs;
        // Для matching правильный ответ хранится как JSON пар
        finalCorrectAnswer = JSON.stringify(filledPairs);
      }

      if (type === "fill_blank") {
        optionsJsonb = [];
      }

      const payload: ExerciseCreate = {
        type,
        question: question.trim(),
        options_jsonb: optionsJsonb,
        correct_answer: finalCorrectAnswer,
        hint: hint.trim() || null,
        audio_file_id: exercise?.audio_file_id || null,
        score_weight: scoreWeight,
      };

      if (exercise) {
        await exerciseApi.update(exercise.id, payload);
      } else {
        await exerciseApi.create(lessonId, payload);
      }

      onSaved();
    } catch (e) {
      setFormError(
        e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Не удалось сохранить упражнение"
      );
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  const renderPreview = () => {
    return (
      <div className="exercise-preview">
        <h4>Предпросмотр упражнения</h4>
        <div className="preview-card">
          <div className="preview-type">
            {EXERCISE_TYPES.find((t) => t.value === type)?.label}
          </div>
          <div className="preview-question">{question || "Вопрос не задан"}</div>

          {type === "translation" || type === "audio_recognition" ? (
            <div className="preview-options">
              {options
                .filter((o) => o.trim() !== "")
                .map((option, index) => (
                  <div key={index} className="preview-option">
                    ○ {option}
                  </div>
                ))}
            </div>
          ) : null}

          {type === "fill_blank" ? (
            <div className="preview-fill">
              <input type="text" placeholder="Ответ ученика" disabled />
            </div>
          ) : null}

          {type === "matching" ? (
            <div className="preview-matching">
              {matchingPairs
                .filter((p) => p.left.trim() !== "" && p.right.trim() !== "")
                .map((pair, index) => (
                  <div key={index} className="preview-pair">
                    <span>{pair.left}</span>
                    <span>→</span>
                    <span>{pair.right}</span>
                  </div>
                ))}
            </div>
          ) : null}

          {hint && <div className="preview-hint">💡 {hint}</div>}
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  };

  return (
    <div className="card exercise-form-card">
      <div className="exercise-form-header">
        <h3>{exercise ? "Редактировать упражнение" : "Новое упражнение"}</h3>
        <button
          type="button"
          className="btn-secondary"
          onClick={() => setShowPreview(!showPreview)}
        >
          {showPreview ? "Скрыть предпросмотр" : "Предпросмотр"}
        </button>
      </div>

      {formError && <div className="error">{formError}</div>}

      <div className="exercise-form-grid">
        <div className="exercise-form-main">
          {/* Тип упражнения */}
          <div className="form-row">
            <label>Тип упражнения</label>
            <div className="type-selector">
              {EXERCISE_TYPES.map((t) => (
                <button
                  key={t.value}
                  type="button"
                  className={`type-option ${type === t.value ? "type-option-active" : ""}`}
                  onClick={() => setType(t.value)}
                >
                  <span className="type-option-label">{t.label}</span>
                  <span className="type-option-description">{t.description}</span>
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Вопрос */}
          <div className="form-row">
            <label>Вопрос / Задание</label>
            <textarea
              value={question}
              onChange={(e) => setQuestion(e.target.value)}
              placeholder={
                type === "fill_blank"
                  ? "Например: Бур ___! (Добрый день!)"
                  : "Например: Переведи слово 'бур' на русский язык"
              }
              rows={3}
            />
            {type === "fill_blank" && (
              <span className="field-hint">
                Используй ___ для обозначения пропуска в тексте
              </span>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Правильный ответ (не для matching) */}
          {needsCorrectAnswer && (
            <div className="form-row">
              <label>Правильный ответ</label>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={correctAnswer}
                onChange={(e) => setCorrectAnswer(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Например: хороший"
              />
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Подсказка */}
          <div className="form-row">
            <label>Подсказка (необязательно)</label>
            <input
              type="text"
              value={hint}
              onChange={(e) => setHint(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Например: Это слово описывает что-то положительное"
            />
          </div>

          {/* Вес */}
          <div className="form-row">
            <label>Вес упражнения (баллы)</label>
            <input
              type="number"
              min={1}
              max={100}
              value={scoreWeight}
              onChange={(e) => setScoreWeight(parseInt(e.target.value, 10) || 1)}
            />
          </div>

          {/* Варианты ответа для translation и audio_recognition */}
          {(type === "translation" || type === "audio_recognition") && (
            <div className="form-section">
              <h4>Варианты ответа</h4>
              <p className="section-hint">
                Добавь минимум 2 варианта. Правильный ответ должен быть среди них.
              </p>
              {options.map((option, index) => (
                <div key={index} className="option-row">
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    value={option}
                    onChange={(e) => handleOptionChange(index, e.target.value)}
                    placeholder={`Вариант ${index + 1}`}
                  />
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    className="btn-small btn-danger"
                    onClick={() => handleRemoveOption(index)}
                    disabled={options.length <= 2}
                  >
                    ×
                  </button>
                </div>
              ))}
              <button type="button" className="btn-secondary" onClick={handleAddOption}>
                + Добавить вариант
              </button>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Пары для matching */}
          {type === "matching" && (
            <div className="form-section">
              <h4>Пары для сопоставления</h4>
              <p className="section-hint">
                Добавь минимум 2 пары. Левая колонка — слово на коми, правая — перевод.
              </p>
              <div className="matching-pairs-header">
                <span>Слово на коми</span>
                <span>Перевод</span>
                <span></span>
              </div>
              {matchingPairs.map((pair, index) => (
                <div key={index} className="matching-pair-row">
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    value={pair.left}
                    onChange={(e) => handlePairChange(index, "left", e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="Слово на коми"
                  />
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    value={pair.right}
                    onChange={(e) => handlePairChange(index, "right", e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="Перевод"
                  />
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    className="btn-small btn-danger"
                    onClick={() => handleRemovePair(index)}
                    disabled={matchingPairs.length <= 1}
                  >
                    ×
                  </button>
                </div>
              ))}
              <button type="button" className="btn-secondary" onClick={handleAddPair}>
                + Добавить пару
              </button>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Предпросмотр */}
        {showPreview && <div className="exercise-form-preview">{renderPreview()}</div>}
      </div>

      <div className="modal-footer">
        <button
          type="button"
          className="btn-secondary"
          onClick={onCancel}
          disabled={submitting}
        >
          Отмена
        </button>
        <button type="button" onClick={handleSubmit} disabled={submitting}>
          {submitting ? "Сохранение..." : "Сохранить"}
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
````
