### Файл: frontend/src/components/ExercisePlayer.tsx


````text
import { useState } from "react";

import { exerciseApi } from "../api/exercises";
import type { ExerciseCheckResult, ExerciseOut, ExerciseType, MatchingPair } from "../types";

interface ExercisePlayerProps {
  exercise: ExerciseOut;
  onComplete: (isCorrect: boolean) => void;
}

const EXERCISE_TYPE_LABELS: Record<ExerciseType, string> = {
  translation: "Перевод",
  matching: "Сопоставление",
  fill_blank: "Заполни пропуск",
  audio_recognition: "Аудио-распознавание",
};

export default function ExercisePlayer({ exercise, onComplete }: ExercisePlayerProps) {
  const [answer, setAnswer] = useState("");
  const [selectedOption, setSelectedOption] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [matchingAnswers, setMatchingAnswers] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [checking, setChecking] = useState(false);
  const [result, setResult] = useState<ExerciseCheckResult | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  const options =
    Array.isArray(exercise.options_jsonb) && typeof exercise.options_jsonb[0] === "string"
      ? (exercise.options_jsonb as string[])
      : [];

  const matchingPairs =
    Array.isArray(exercise.options_jsonb) &&
    exercise.options_jsonb.length > 0 &&
    typeof exercise.options_jsonb[0] === "object"
      ? (exercise.options_jsonb as MatchingPair[])
      : [];

  const canSubmit = () => {
    if (exercise.type === "translation" || exercise.type === "audio_recognition") {
      return selectedOption !== null || answer.trim() !== "";
    }
    if (exercise.type === "fill_blank") {
      return answer.trim() !== "";
    }
    if (exercise.type === "matching") {
      return Object.keys(matchingAnswers).length === matchingPairs.length;
    }
    return false;
  };

  const handleCheck = async () => {
    setChecking(true);
    setError("");
    setResult(null);

    try {
      let finalAnswer = "";

      if (exercise.type === "translation" || exercise.type === "audio_recognition") {
        finalAnswer = selectedOption || answer;
      }

      if (exercise.type === "fill_blank") {
        finalAnswer = answer;
      }

      if (exercise.type === "matching") {
        finalAnswer = JSON.stringify(matchingAnswers);
      }

      const checkResult = await exerciseApi.check(exercise.id, {
        answer: finalAnswer,
      });

      setResult(checkResult);
      onComplete(checkResult.is_correct);
    } catch (e) {
      setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Ошибка проверки ответа");
    } finally {
      setChecking(false);
    }
  };

  const handleReset = () => {
    setAnswer("");
    setSelectedOption(null);
    setMatchingAnswers({});
    setResult(null);
    setError("");
  };

  const renderInput = () => {
    switch (exercise.type) {
      case "translation":
      case "audio_recognition":
        return (
          <div className="exercise-options">
            {options.length > 0 ? (
              options.map((option, index) => (
                <label key={index} className="option-label">
                  <input
                    type="radio"
                    name={`exercise-${exercise.id}`}
                    value={option}
                    checked={selectedOption === option}
                    onChange={() => setSelectedOption(option)}
                  />
                  <span>{option}</span>
                </label>
              ))
            ) : (
              <input
                type="text"
                value={answer}
                onChange={(e) => setAnswer(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Введи ответ"
                className="exercise-input"
              />
            )}
          </div>
        );

      case "fill_blank":
        return (
          <div className="form-row">
            <input
              type="text"
              value={answer}
              onChange={(e) => setAnswer(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Вставь пропущенное слово"
              className="exercise-input"
            />
          </div>
        );

      case "matching":
        return (
          <div className="matching-container">
            <div className="matching-header">
              <span>Слово</span>
              <span>Выбери перевод</span>
            </div>
            {matchingPairs.map((pair, index) => (
              <div key={index} className="matching-row">
                <span className="matching-left">{pair.left}</span>
                <select
                  value={matchingAnswers[pair.left] || ""}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setMatchingAnswers({
                      ...matchingAnswers,
                      [pair.left]: e.target.value,
                    })
                  }
                  className="matching-select"
                >
                  <option value="">Выбери перевод</option>
                  {/* Перемешиваем правые варианты */}
                  {[...matchingPairs]
                    .sort(() => 0.5 - Math.random())
                    .map((p, i) => (
                      <option key={i} value={p.right}>
                        {p.right}
                      </option>
                    ))}
                </select>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        );

      default:
        return null;
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="exercise-card">
      <div className="exercise-header">
        <span className="exercise-type-badge">
          {EXERCISE_TYPE_LABELS[exercise.type]}
        </span>
        <span className="exercise-weight">Вес: {exercise.score_weight}</span>
      </div>

      <div className="exercise-question">
        <p>{exercise.question}</p>
      </div>

      {error && <div className="error">{error}</div>}

      {renderInput()}

      <div className="exercise-actions">
        <button type="button" onClick={handleCheck} disabled={checking || !canSubmit()}>
          {checking ? "Проверяю..." : "Проверить"}
        </button>

        {result && (
          <button type="button" className="btn-secondary" onClick={handleReset}>
            Попробовать снова
          </button>
        )}
      </div>

      {result && (
        <div className={`exercise-result ${result.is_correct ? "result-correct" : "result-incorrect"}`}>
          <p>
            <strong>{result.is_correct ? "✅ Правильно!" : "❌ Неправильно"}</strong>
          </p>
          {result.message && <p>{result.message}</p>}
          {result.hint && !result.is_correct && (
            <p className="exercise-hint">💡 Подсказка: {result.hint}</p>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
````

````text
import { Link, useLocation } from "react-router-dom";
import { useAuth } from "../auth";

interface SidebarProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
}

interface MenuItem {
  path: string;
  label: string;
  icon: string;
  roles: Array<"student" | "teacher" | "admin">;
  external?: boolean;
}

const MENU_ITEMS: MenuItem[] = [
  { path: "/", label: "Главная", icon: "🏠", roles: ["student", "teacher", "admin"] },
  { path: "/modules", label: "Модули", icon: "📚", roles: ["student", "teacher", "admin"] },
  { path: "/dashboard", label: "Дашборд", icon: "📊", roles: ["student"] },
  { path: "/admin-dashboard", label: "Админ-дашборд", icon: "📈", roles: ["admin"] },
  { path: "/progress", label: "Прогресс", icon: "📈", roles: ["student"] },
  { path: "/achievements", label: "Достижения", icon: "🏆", roles: ["student"] },
  { path: "/vocabulary", label: "Словарь", icon: "📖", roles: ["student", "teacher", "admin"] },
  { path: "/categories", label: "Категории", icon: "🏷️", roles: ["student", "teacher", "admin"] },
  { path: "/profile", label: "Профиль", icon: "👤", roles: ["student", "teacher", "admin"] },
  { path: "/admin", label: "Админ-панель", icon: "⚙️", roles: ["admin"], external: true },
];

export default function Sidebar({ isOpen, onClose }: SidebarProps) {
  const { user, logout } = useAuth();
  const location = useLocation();

  if (!user) return null;

  const filteredItems = MENU_ITEMS.filter((item) =>
    item.roles.includes(user.role)
  );

  return (
    <>
      {/* Оверлей */}
      {isOpen && <div className="sidebar-overlay" onClick={onClose} />}

      {/* Боковое меню */}
      <aside className={`sidebar ${isOpen ? "sidebar-open" : ""}`}>
        <div className="sidebar-header">
          <span className="sidebar-brand">Коми кыв</span>
          <button type="button" className="sidebar-close" onClick={onClose}>
            ×
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="sidebar-user">
          <div className="sidebar-user-avatar">
            {user.full_name?.charAt(0) || user.email.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}
          </div>
          <div className="sidebar-user-info">
            <div className="sidebar-user-name">
              {user.full_name || user.email}
            </div>
            <div className="sidebar-user-role">
              {user.role === "student" && "Ученик"}
              {user.role === "teacher" && "Преподаватель"}
              {user.role === "admin" && "Администратор"}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <nav className="sidebar-nav">
          {filteredItems.map((item) => {
            if (item.external) {
              return (
                <a
                  key={item.path}
                  href={item.path}
                  className="sidebar-link"
                  onClick={onClose}
                >
                  <span className="sidebar-link-icon">{item.icon}</span>
                  <span>{item.label}</span>
                </a>
              );
            }

            return (
              <Link
                key={item.path}
                to={item.path}
                className={`sidebar-link ${
                  location.pathname === item.path ? "sidebar-link-active" : ""
                }`}
                onClick={onClose}
              >
                <span className="sidebar-link-icon">{item.icon}</span>
                <span>{item.label}</span>
              </Link>
            );
          })}
        </nav>

        <div className="sidebar-footer">
          <button type="button" className="sidebar-logout" onClick={logout}>
            🚪 Выйти
          </button>
        </div>
      </aside>
    </>
  );
}
````

````text
import { useState } from "react";

import { vocabularyApi } from "../api/exercises";
import type { VocabularyCreate, VocabularyOut } from "../types";

interface VocabularyFormProps {
  lessonId: number;
  vocabularyItem?: VocabularyOut;
  onSaved: () => void;
  onCancel: () => void;
}

export default function VocabularyForm({
  lessonId,
  vocabularyItem,
  onSaved,
  onCancel,
}: VocabularyFormProps) {
  const [wordKomi, setWordKomi] = useState(
    vocabularyItem?.word_komi || ""
  );
  const [translationRu, setTranslationRu] = useState(
    vocabularyItem?.translation_ru || ""
  );
  const [transcription, setTranscription] = useState(
    vocabularyItem?.transcription || ""
  );
  const [exampleText, setExampleText] = useState(
    vocabularyItem?.example_text || ""
  );

  const [formError, setFormError] = useState("");
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);

  const validate = (): string | null => {
    if (!wordKomi.trim()) {
      return "Слово на коми не может быть пустым";
    }

    if (!translationRu.trim()) {
      return "Перевод не может быть пустым";
    }

    return null;
  };

  const handleSubmit = async () => {
    const validationError = validate();
    if (validationError) {
      setFormError(validationError);
      return;
    }

    setSubmitting(true);
    setFormError("");

    try {
      const payload: VocabularyCreate = {
        word_komi: wordKomi.trim(),
        translation_ru: translationRu.trim(),
        transcription: transcription.trim() || null,
        example_text: exampleText.trim() || null,
        audio_file_id: vocabularyItem?.audio_file_id || null,
      };

      if (vocabularyItem) {
        await vocabularyApi.update(vocabularyItem.id, payload);
      } else {
        await vocabularyApi.create(lessonId, payload);
      }

      onSaved();
    } catch (e) {
      setFormError(
        e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Не удалось сохранить словарную статью"
      );
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="card">
      <h3>
        {vocabularyItem ? "Редактировать слово" : "Новое словарное слово"}
      </h3>

      {formError && <div className="error">{formError}</div>}

      <div className="grid grid-2">
        <div className="form-row">
          <label>Слово на коми</label>
          <input
            type="text"
            value={wordKomi}
            onChange={(e) => setWordKomi(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Например: бур"
          />
        </div>

        <div className="form-row">
          <label>Перевод на русский</label>
          <input
            type="text"
            value={translationRu}
            onChange={(e) => setTranslationRu(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Например: хороший"
          />
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="form-row">
        <label>Транскрипция (необязательно)</label>
        <input
          type="text"
          value={transcription}
          onChange={(e) => setTranscription(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="Например: [бур]"
        />
      </div>

      <div className="form-row">
        <label>Пример использования (необязательно)</label>
        <textarea
          value={exampleText}
          onChange={(e) => setExampleText(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="Например: Бур лун! — Добрый день!"
          rows={3}
        />
      </div>

      <div className="modal-footer">
        <button
          type="button"
          className="btn-secondary"
          onClick={onCancel}
          disabled={submitting}
        >
          Отмена
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={handleSubmit}
          disabled={submitting}
        >
          {submitting ? "Сохранение..." : "Сохранить"}
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
````

````text
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

import { progressApi } from "../api/progress";
import type { UserAchievementOut } from "../types";

export default function AchievementsPage() {
  const [achievements, setAchievements] = useState<UserAchievementOut[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;

    const load = async () => {
      try {
        setLoading(true);
        setError("");

        const data = await progressApi.getAchievements();

        if (!cancelled) {
          setAchievements(data);
        }
      } catch (e) {
        if (!cancelled) {
          setError(
            e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Не удалось загрузить достижения"
          );
        }
      } finally {
        if (!cancelled) {
          setLoading(false);
        }
      }
    };

    load();

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, []);

  return (
    <div className="container">
      <h1>Мои достижения</h1>

      {error && <div className="card error">{error}</div>}

      <div className="card">
        {loading ? (
          <p>Загрузка...</p>
        ) : achievements.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="muted">
            У тебя пока нет достижений. Проходи уроки, чтобы получать награды!
          </p>
        ) : (
          <div className="achievements-grid">
            {achievements.map((ua) => (
              <div key={ua.achievement.id} className="achievement-card">
                <div className="achievement-card-icon">🏆</div>
                <h3 className="achievement-card-title">
                  {ua.achievement.title}
                </h3>
                <p className="achievement-card-description">
                  {ua.achievement.description}
                </p>
                <div className="achievement-card-footer">
                  <span className="achievement-card-xp">
                    +{ua.achievement.xp_reward} XP
                  </span>
                  <span className="achievement-card-date">
                    {new Date(ua.received_at).toLocaleDateString("ru-RU")}
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
````

````text
  rejected: "#ef4444",
  published: "#3b82f6",
  archived: "#6b7280",
};

};

        setLoading(true);

        setData(result);

      }
    };
    load();

  if (loading) {
````

````text
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";

import { categoryApi } from "../api/vocabulary";
import type { CategoryPage } from "../types";

export default function CategoriesPage() {
  const [data, setData] = useState<CategoryPage | null>(null);
  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  const pageSize = 20;

  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      setLoading(true);
      setError("");

      const result = await categoryApi.list(page, pageSize);

      setData(result);
    } catch (e) {
      setError(
        e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Не удалось загрузить категории"
      );
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, [page]);

  useEffect(() => {
    load();
  }, [load]);

  const totalPages = data
    ? Math.max(1, Math.ceil(data.total / data.page_size))
    : 1;

  return (
    <div className="container">
      <h1>Категории модулей</h1>

      {error && <div className="card error">{error}</div>}

      <div className="card">
        {loading ? (
          <p>Загрузка...</p>
        ) : !data || data.items.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="muted">
            Категории пока не созданы. Администратор может создать их через
            административную панель.
          </p>
        ) : (
          <>
            <table>
              <thead>
                <tr>
                  <th>Название</th>
                  <th>Slug</th>
                  <th>Описание</th>
                  <th>Создана</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {data.items.map((item) => (
                  <tr key={item.id}>
                    <td>
                      <strong>{item.name}</strong>
                    </td>
                    <td>{item.slug}</td>
                    <td>{item.description || "—"}</td>
                    <td>
                      {new Date(item.created_at).toLocaleDateString("ru-RU")}
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>

            <div className="pagination">
              <button
                type="button"
                disabled={page <= 1}
                onClick={() => setPage((p) => p - 1)}
              >
                ← Назад
              </button>

              <span>
                Страница {data.page} из {totalPages}
              </span>

              <button
                type="button"
                disabled={page >= totalPages}
                onClick={() => setPage((p) => p + 1)}
              >
                Вперёд →
              </button>
            </div>
          </>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
````


### Файл: frontend/src/pages/DashboardPage.tsx


````text
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

import { progressApi } from "../api/progress";
import type { ProgressSummary, UserAchievementOut } from "../types";

export default function DashboardPage() {
  const [summary, setSummary] = useState<ProgressSummary | null>(null);
  const [achievements, setAchievements] = useState<UserAchievementOut[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;

    const load = async () => {
      try {
        setLoading(true);
        setError("");

        const [summaryData, achievementsData] = await Promise.all([
          progressApi.getSummary(),
          progressApi.getAchievements(),
        ]);

        if (!cancelled) {
          setSummary(summaryData);
          setAchievements(achievementsData);
        }
      } catch (e) {
        if (!cancelled) {
          setError(
            e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Не удалось загрузить данные"
          );
        }
      } finally {
        if (!cancelled) {
          setLoading(false);
        }
      }
    };

    load();

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, []);

  if (loading) {
    return <div className="container">Загрузка дашборда...</div>;
  }

  if (error) {
    return (
      <div className="container">
        <div className="card error">Ошибка: {error}</div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (!summary) {
    return null;
  }

  const nextLevelXp = (summary.xp + 500) - (summary.xp % 500);
  const currentLevelXp = summary.xp - (summary.xp % 500);
  const progressPercent = Math.min(
    100,
    Math.round(((summary.xp - currentLevelXp) / (nextLevelXp - currentLevelXp)) * 100)
  );

  return (
    <div className="container">
      <h1>Мой прогресс</h1>

      <div className="grid grid-2">
        <div className="card">
          <h2>Общий прогресс</h2>

          <div className="progress-stats">
            <div className="stat">
              <span className="stat-label">Уровень</span>
              <span className="stat-value">{summary.level}</span>
            </div>

            <div className="stat">
              <span className="stat-label">Опыт (XP)</span>
              <span className="stat-value">{summary.xp}</span>
            </div>

            <div className="stat">
              <span className="stat-label">Серия дней</span>
              <span className="stat-value">
                {summary.current_streak} {summary.current_streak === 1 ? "день" : "дней"}
              </span>
            </div>

            <div className="stat">
              <span className="stat-label">Пройдено уроков</span>
              <span className="stat-value">{summary.completed_lessons}</span>
            </div>

            <div className="stat">
              <span className="stat-label">Достижений</span>
              <span className="stat-value">{summary.total_achievements}</span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="progress-bar-wrapper">
            <div className="progress-bar-header">
              <span>Прогресс до следующего уровня</span>
              <span>{progressPercent}%</span>
            </div>
            <div className="progress-bar">
              <div
                className="progress-bar-fill"
                style={{ width: `${progressPercent}%` }}
              />
            </div>
            <div className="progress-bar-footer">
              {summary.xp} / {nextLevelXp} XP
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="card">
          <h2>Последние достижения</h2>

          {achievements.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="muted">
              У тебя пока нет достижений. Пройди свой первый урок!
            </p>
          ) : (
            <ul className="achievement-list">
              {achievements.slice(0, 5).map((ua) => (
                <li key={ua.achievement.id} className="achievement-item">
                  <div className="achievement-icon">🏆</div>
                  <div className="achievement-info">
                    <div className="achievement-title">
                      {ua.achievement.title}
                    </div>
                    <div className="achievement-description">
                      {ua.achievement.description}
                    </div>
                    <div className="achievement-meta">
                      +{ua.achievement.xp_reward} XP •{" "}
                      {new Date(ua.received_at).toLocaleDateString("ru-RU")}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
````

````text
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Link, useParams } from "react-router-dom";

import { exerciseApi, vocabularyApi } from "../api/exercises";
import { lessonApi } from "../api/lessons";
import ExerciseForm from "../components/ExerciseForm";
import ExercisePlayer from "../components/ExercisePlayer";
import VocabularyForm from "../components/VocabularyForm";
import type {
  ExerciseOut,
  LessonOut,
  VocabularyOut,
} from "../types";
import { useAuth } from "../auth";

type TabType = "theory" | "exercises" | "vocabulary";

export default function LessonPage() {
  const { lessonId } = useParams<{ lessonId: string }>();
  const { user } = useAuth();

  const [lesson, setLesson] = useState<LessonOut | null>(null);
  const [exercises, setExercises] = useState<ExerciseOut[]>([]);
  const [vocabulary, setVocabulary] = useState<VocabularyOut[]>([]);
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<TabType>("theory");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  // Конструктор упражнений
  const [isExerciseFormOpen, setIsExerciseFormOpen] = useState(false);
  const [editingExercise, setEditingExercise] = useState<ExerciseOut | undefined>();

  // Словарь
  const [isVocabularyFormOpen, setIsVocabularyFormOpen] = useState(false);
  const [editingVocabulary, setEditingVocabulary] = useState<VocabularyOut | undefined>();

  // Прохождение
  const [completedExercises, setCompletedExercises] = useState<Set<number>>(new Set());

  const canEdit = user?.role === "teacher" || user?.role === "admin";

  const loadLesson = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!lessonId) return;

    try {
      setLoading(true);
      setError("");

      const lessonData = await lessonApi.getLesson(Number(lessonId));
      setLesson(lessonData);

      const exercisesData = await exerciseApi.list(Number(lessonId), 1, 100);
      setExercises(exercisesData.items);

      const vocabularyData = await vocabularyApi.list(Number(lessonId), 1, 100);
      setVocabulary(vocabularyData.items);
    } catch (e) {
      setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Не удалось загрузить урок");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, [lessonId]);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadLesson();
  }, [loadLesson]);

  const openExerciseForm = (exercise?: ExerciseOut) => {
    setEditingExercise(exercise);
    setIsExerciseFormOpen(true);
  };

  const closeExerciseForm = () => {
    setIsExerciseFormOpen(false);
    setEditingExercise(undefined);
  };

  const openVocabularyForm = (item?: VocabularyOut) => {
    setEditingVocabulary(item);
    setIsVocabularyFormOpen(true);
  };

  const closeVocabularyForm = () => {
    setIsVocabularyFormOpen(false);
    setEditingVocabulary(undefined);
  };

  const handleDeleteExercise = async (exerciseId: number) => {
    if (!confirm("Удалить это упражнение?")) return;

    try {
      await exerciseApi.delete(exerciseId);
      await loadLesson();
    } catch (e) {
      setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Не удалось удалить упражнение");
    }
  };

  const handleDeleteVocabulary = async (vocabularyId: number) => {
    if (!confirm("Удалить эту словарную статью?")) return;

    try {
      await vocabularyApi.delete(vocabularyId);
      await loadLesson();
    } catch (e) {
      setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Не удалось удалить статью");
    }
  };

  const handleExerciseComplete = (exerciseId: number, isCorrect: boolean) => {
    if (isCorrect) {
      setCompletedExercises((prev) => new Set(prev).add(exerciseId));
    }
  };

  if (loading) {
    return <div className="container">Загрузка урока...</div>;
  }

  if (error) {
    return (
      <div className="container">
        <div className="card error">{error}</div>
        <Link to="/modules" className="btn-secondary">
          ← Вернуться к модулям
        </Link>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (!lesson) {
    return null;
  }

  return (
    <div className="container">
      <div className="breadcrumb">
        <Link to="/modules">Модули</Link>
        <span> / </span>
        <Link to={`/modules/${lesson.module_id}`}>Модуль</Link>
        <span> / </span>
        <span>{lesson.title}</span>
      </div>

      <div className="card">
        <h1>{lesson.title}</h1>

        <div className="lesson-meta">
          <span>Проходной балл: {lesson.passing_score}%</span>
          <span>
            Попытки:{" "}
            {lesson.max_attempts === null ? "∞" : lesson.max_attempts}
          </span>
          <span>
            Пройдено упражнений: {completedExercises.size}/{exercises.length}
          </span>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="tabs">
        <button
          type="button"
          className={activeTab === "theory" ? "tab active" : "tab"}
          onClick={() => setActiveTab("theory")}
        >
          Теория
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          className={activeTab === "exercises" ? "tab active" : "tab"}
          onClick={() => setActiveTab("exercises")}
        >
          Упражнения ({exercises.length})
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          className={activeTab === "vocabulary" ? "tab active" : "tab"}
          onClick={() => setActiveTab("vocabulary")}
        >
          Словарь ({vocabulary.length})
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Вкладка: Теория */}
      {activeTab === "theory" && (
        <div className="card">
          <h2>Теоретический материал</h2>
          {lesson.theory_content ? (
            <div className="theory-content">
              <p>{lesson.theory_content}</p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <p className="muted">
              Теоретический материал пока не добавлен.
            </p>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Вкладка: Упражнения */}
      {activeTab === "exercises" && (
        <div className="card">
          <div className="section-header">
            <h2>Упражнения</h2>

            {canEdit && (
              <button type="button" onClick={() => openExerciseForm()}>
                + Добавить упражнение
              </button>
            )}
          </div>

          {isExerciseFormOpen && (
            <ExerciseForm
              lessonId={Number(lessonId)}
              exercise={editingExercise}
              onSaved={() => {
                closeExerciseForm();
                loadLesson();
              }}
              onCancel={closeExerciseForm}
            />
          )}

          {exercises.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="muted">
              В уроке пока нет упражнений.{" "}
              {canEdit && "Добавь первое упражнение."}
            </p>
          ) : (
            <div className="exercises-list">
              {exercises.map((exercise) => (
                <div key={exercise.id} className="exercise-wrapper">
                  {completedExercises.has(exercise.id) && (
                    <div className="exercise-completed-badge">✅ Пройдено</div>
                  )}

                  <ExercisePlayer
                    exercise={exercise}
                    onComplete={(isCorrect) =>
                      handleExerciseComplete(exercise.id, isCorrect)
                    }
                  />

                  {canEdit && (
                    <div className="exercise-admin-actions">
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        className="btn-small"
                        onClick={() => openExerciseForm(exercise)}
                      >
                        Изменить
                      </button>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        className="btn-small btn-danger"
                        onClick={() => handleDeleteExercise(exercise.id)}
                      >
                        Удалить
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Вкладка: Словарь */}
      {activeTab === "vocabulary" && (
        <div className="card">
          <div className="section-header">
            <h2>Словарь урока</h2>

            {canEdit && (
              <button type="button" onClick={() => openVocabularyForm()}>
                + Добавить слово
              </button>
            )}
          </div>

          {isVocabularyFormOpen && (
            <VocabularyForm
              lessonId={Number(lessonId)}
              vocabularyItem={editingVocabulary}
              onSaved={() => {
                closeVocabularyForm();
                loadLesson();
              }}
              onCancel={closeVocabularyForm}
            />
          )}

          {vocabulary.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="muted">
              В уроке пока нет словарных статей.{" "}
              {canEdit && "Добавь первое слово."}
            </p>
          ) : (
            <table>
              <thead>
                <tr>
                  <th>Слово на коми</th>
                  <th>Перевод</th>
                  <th>Транскрипция</th>
                  <th>Пример</th>
                  {canEdit && <th>Действия</th>}
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {vocabulary.map((item) => (
                  <tr key={item.id}>
                    <td>
                      <strong>{item.word_komi}</strong>
                    </td>
                    <td>{item.translation_ru}</td>
                    <td>{item.transcription || "—"}</td>
                    <td>{item.example_text || "—"}</td>
                    {canEdit && (
                      <td>
                        <div className="actions">
                          <button
                            type="button"
                            className="btn-small"
                            onClick={() => openVocabularyForm(item)}
                          >
                            Изменить
                          </button>
                          <button
                            type="button"
                            className="btn-small btn-danger"
                            onClick={() => handleDeleteVocabulary(item.id)}
                          >
                            Удалить
                          </button>
                        </div>
                      </td>
                    )}
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
````


### Файл: frontend/src/pages/LoginPage.tsx


````text
import { useState } from "react";
import { useForm } from "react-hook-form";
import { zodResolver } from "@hookform/resolvers/zod";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { z } from "zod";

import { useAuth } from "../auth";

const schema = z.object({
  email: z.string().email("Укажи корректный email"),
  password: z.string().min(8, "Пароль должен содержать минимум 8 символов")
});

type FormData = z.infer<typeof schema>;

export default function LoginPage() {
  const { login } = useAuth();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const [serverError, setServerError] = useState("");

  const {
    register,
    handleSubmit,
    formState: { errors, isSubmitting }
  } = useForm<FormData>({
    resolver: zodResolver(schema),
    defaultValues: {
      email: "",
      password: ""
    }
  });

  const onSubmit = async (data: FormData) => {
    setServerError("");

    try {
      await login(data.email, data.password);
      navigate("/modules");
    } catch (error) {
      setServerError(
        error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Не удалось войти"
      );
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="container">
      <div className="card auth-card">
        <h1>Вход</h1>

        {serverError && <p className="error">{serverError}</p>}

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)}>
          <div className="form-row">
            <label htmlFor="email">Email</label>
            <input id="email" type="email" {...register("email")} />
            {errors.email && (
              <span className="error">{errors.email.message}</span>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="form-row">
            <label htmlFor="password">Пароль</label>
            <input id="password" type="password" {...register("password")} />
            {errors.password && (
              <span className="error">{errors.password.message}</span>
            )}
          </div>

          <button type="submit" disabled={isSubmitting}>
            {isSubmitting ? "Выполняется вход..." : "Войти"}
          </button>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
````


### Файл: frontend/src/pages/ModuleDetailPage.tsx


````text
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Link, useParams } from "react-router-dom";

import { lessonApi } from "../api/lessons";
import type { LessonCreate, LessonOut, LessonUpdate, ModuleOut } from "../types";
import { useAuth } from "../auth";

const EMPTY_FORM: LessonCreate = {
  title: "",
  order_index: 0,
  theory_content: "",
  passing_score: 70,
  max_attempts: null,
};

export default function ModuleDetailPage() {
  const { moduleId } = useParams<{ moduleId: string }>();
  const { user } = useAuth();

  const [module, setModule] = useState<ModuleOut | null>(null);
  const [lessons, setLessons] = useState<LessonOut[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  // Форма создания/редактирования урока
  const [isFormOpen, setIsFormOpen] = useState(false);
  const [editingLessonId, setEditingLessonId] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [form, setForm] = useState<LessonCreate>(EMPTY_FORM);
  const [formError, setFormError] = useState("");
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  
  // Статусы и удаление
  const [statusChanging, setStatusChanging] = useState(false);
  const [deleting, setDeleting] = useState(false);

  const canEdit = user?.role === "teacher" || user?.role === "admin";
  const isAdmin = user?.role === "admin";

  const getAvailableActions = () => {
    if (!module || !user) return [];

    const actions: Array<{
      key: string;
      label: string;
      color: string;
    }> = [];

    const status = module.status;
    const isAuthor = module.author_id === user.id;

    if (status === "draft") {
      if (user.role === "teacher" && isAuthor) {
        actions.push({
          key: "submit",
          label: "Отправить на модерацию",
          color: "#2563eb",
        });
      }
    }

    if (status === "moderation") {
      if (user.role === "teacher" && isAuthor) {
        actions.push({
          key: "withdraw",
          label: "Отозвать с модерации",
          color: "#6b7280",
        });
      }

      if (isAdmin) {
        actions.push({
          key: "approve",
          label: "Одобрить",
          color: "#059669",
        });
        actions.push({
          key: "reject",
          label: "Отклонить",
          color: "#dc2626",
        });
      }
    }

    if (status === "rejected") {
      if (isAdmin || (user.role === "teacher" && isAuthor)) {
        actions.push({
          key: "submit",
          label: "Вернуть на модерацию",
          color: "#2563eb",
        });
      }
    }

    if (status === "approved") {
      if (isAdmin || (user.role === "teacher" && isAuthor)) {
        actions.push({
          key: "publish",
          label: "Опубликовать",
          color: "#059669",
        });
      }
    }

    if (status === "published") {
      if (isAdmin) {
        actions.push({
          key: "archive",
          label: "Архивировать",
          color: "#6b7280",
        });
      }
      
      // Снять с публикации может админ или автор
      if (isAdmin || (user.role === "teacher" && isAuthor)) {
        actions.push({
          key: "unpublish",
          label: "Снять с публикации",
          color: "#ea580c",
        });
      }
    }
    return actions;
  };

  const loadModule = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!moduleId) return;

    try {
      setLoading(true);
      setError("");

      const moduleData = await lessonApi.getModule(Number(moduleId));
      const lessonsData = await lessonApi.listLessons(Number(moduleId), 1, 100);

      setModule(moduleData);
      setLessons(lessonsData.items);
    } catch (e) {
      setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Не удалось загрузить модуль");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, [moduleId]);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadModule();
  }, [loadModule]);

  const openCreateForm = () => {
    setEditingLessonId(null);
    setForm({
      ...EMPTY_FORM,
      order_index:
        lessons.length > 0
          ? Math.max(...lessons.map((l) => l.order_index)) + 1
          : 0,
    });
    setFormError("");
    setIsFormOpen(true);
  };

  const openEditForm = (lesson: LessonOut) => {
    setEditingLessonId(lesson.id);
    setForm({
      title: lesson.title,
      order_index: lesson.order_index,
      theory_content: lesson.theory_content || "",
      passing_score: lesson.passing_score,
      max_attempts: lesson.max_attempts,
    });
    setFormError("");
    setIsFormOpen(true);
  };

  const closeForm = () => {
    setIsFormOpen(false);
    setEditingLessonId(null);
    setForm(EMPTY_FORM);
    setFormError("");
  };

  const handleSubmitLesson = async () => {
    if (!moduleId) return;

    if (!form.title.trim()) {
      setFormError("Название урока не может быть пустым");
      return;
    }

    if (form.passing_score < 0 || form.passing_score > 100) {
      setFormError("Проходной балл должен быть от 0 до 100");
      return;
    }

    if (form.order_index < 0) {
      setFormError("Порядковый номер не может быть отрицательным");
      return;
    }

    try {
      setSubmitting(true);
      setFormError("");

      const payload = {
        title: form.title.trim(),
        order_index: form.order_index,
        theory_content: form.theory_content?.trim() || null,
        passing_score: form.passing_score,
        max_attempts: form.max_attempts,
      };

      if (editingLessonId) {
        await lessonApi.updateLesson(editingLessonId, payload as LessonUpdate);
      } else {
        await lessonApi.createLesson(Number(moduleId), payload);
      }

      closeForm();
      await loadModule();
    } catch (e) {
      setFormError(
        e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Не удалось сохранить урок"
      );
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  const handleDeleteLesson = async (lessonId: number) => {
    if (!confirm("Удалить этот урок? Это действие нельзя отменить.")) {
      return;
    }

    try {
      await lessonApi.deleteLesson(lessonId);
      await loadModule();
    } catch (e) {
      setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Не удалось удалить урок");
    }
  };

  const handleStatusChange = async (action: string) => {
    if (!module) return;

    const confirmMessages: Record<string, string> = {
      submit: "Отправить модуль на модерацию?",
      approve: "Одобрить модуль?",
      reject: "Отклонить модуль?",
      publish: "Опубликовать модуль? Он станет доступен ученикам.",
      archive: "Архивировать модуль? Он будет скрыт из каталога.",
      withdraw: "Отозвать модуль с модерации? Он вернётся в черновики.",
      unpublish: "Снять модуль с публикации? Он вернётся в черновики и станет недоступен ученикам.",
    };

    if (!confirm(confirmMessages[action] || "Вы уверены?")) {
      return;
    }

    try {
      setStatusChanging(true);
      setError("");

      switch (action) {
        case "submit":
          await lessonApi.submitModule(module.id);
          break;
        case "approve":
          await lessonApi.approveModule(module.id);
          break;
        case "reject":
          await lessonApi.rejectModule(module.id);
          break;
        case "publish":
          await lessonApi.publishModule(module.id);
          break;
        case "archive":
          await lessonApi.archiveModule(module.id);
          break;
        case "withdraw":
          await lessonApi.withdrawModule(module.id);
          break;
        case "unpublish":
          await lessonApi.unpublishModule(module.id);
          break;
      }

      await loadModule();
    } catch (e) {
      setError(
        e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Не удалось изменить статус модуля"
      );
    } finally {
      setStatusChanging(false);
    }
  };

  const handleDeleteModule = async () => {
    if (!module) return;

    if (!confirm("Удалить этот модуль? Это действие нельзя отменить.")) {
      return;
    }

    try {
      setDeleting(true);
      setError("");
      await lessonApi.deleteModule(module.id);
      window.location.href = "/modules";
    } catch (e) {
      setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Не удалось удалить модуль");
    } finally {
      setDeleting(false);
    }
  };

  if (loading) {
    return <div className="container">Загрузка модуля...</div>;
  }

  if (error && !module) {
    return (
      <div className="container">
        <div className="card error">{error}</div>
        <Link to="/modules" className="btn-secondary">
          ← Вернуться к модулям
        </Link>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (!module) {
    return null;
  }

  const availableActions = getAvailableActions();

  return (
    <div className="container">
      <div className="breadcrumb">
        <Link to="/modules">Модули</Link>
        <span> / </span>
        <span>{module.title}</span>
      </div>

      <div className="card">
        <div className="module-header">
          <div>
            <h1>{module.title}</h1>
            {module.description && <p className="muted">{module.description}</p>}
          </div>

          <div className="module-meta">
            <span className={`badge badge-status-${module.status}`}>
              {module.status}
            </span>
            <span className="badge badge-level">{module.level}</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="module-info">
          <div className="info-item">
            <span className="info-label">Уровень</span>
            <span className="info-value">{module.level}</span>
          </div>
          <div className="info-item">
            <span className="info-label">Статус</span>
            <span className="info-value">{module.status}</span>
          </div>
          <div className="info-item">
            <span className="info-label">Дата создания</span>
            <span className="info-value">
              {new Date(module.created_at).toLocaleDateString("ru-RU")}
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>

        {availableActions.length > 0 && (
          <div className="status-actions">
            <h3>Управление статусом</h3>
            <div className="status-buttons">
              {availableActions.map((action) => (
                <button
                  key={action.key}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => handleStatusChange(action.key)}
                  disabled={statusChanging}
                  style={{ backgroundColor: action.color }}
                  className="status-action-btn"
                >
                  {statusChanging ? "Обработка..." : action.label}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {(isAdmin || (user?.role === "teacher" && module.author_id === user?.id)) &&
          module.status !== "archived" && (
            <div className="module-delete-section">
              <button
                type="button"
                className="btn-danger btn-large"
                onClick={handleDeleteModule}
                disabled={deleting}
              >
                {deleting ? "Удаление..." : "Удалить модуль"}
              </button>
            </div>
          )}
      </div>

      <div className="card">
        <div className="section-header">
          <h2>Уроки ({lessons.length})</h2>

          {canEdit && module.status !== "archived" && (
            <button type="button" onClick={openCreateForm}>
              + Добавить урок
            </button>
          )}
        </div>

        {lessons.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="muted">
            В модуле пока нет уроков.{" "}
            {canEdit &&
              module.status !== "archived" &&
              "Нажми «Добавить урок», чтобы создать первый."}
          </p>
        ) : (
          <table>
            <thead>
              <tr>
                <th>#</th>
                <th>Название</th>
                <th>Проходной балл</th>
                <th>Попытки</th>
                <th>Создан</th>
                {canEdit && module.status !== "archived" && <th>Действия</th>}
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {lessons.map((lesson) => (
                <tr key={lesson.id}>
                  <td>{lesson.order_index + 1}</td>
                  <td>
                    <Link to={`/lessons/${lesson.id}`}>{lesson.title}</Link>
                  </td>
                  <td>{lesson.passing_score}%</td>
                  <td>
                    {lesson.max_attempts === null ? "∞" : lesson.max_attempts}
                  </td>
                  <td>
                    {new Date(lesson.created_at).toLocaleDateString("ru-RU")}
                  </td>
                  {canEdit && module.status !== "archived" && (
                    <td>
                      <div className="actions">
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          className="btn-small"
                          onClick={() => openEditForm(lesson)}
                        >
                          Изменить
                        </button>
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          className="btn-small btn-danger"
                          onClick={() => handleDeleteLesson(lesson.id)}
                        >
                          Удалить
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    </td>
                  )}
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        )}
      </div>

      {isFormOpen && (
        <div className="modal-overlay" onClick={closeForm}>
          <div className="modal" onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
            <div className="modal-header">
              <h3>{editingLessonId ? "Редактировать урок" : "Новый урок"}</h3>
              <button type="button" className="btn-close" onClick={closeForm}>
                ×
              </button>
            </div>

            {formError && <div className="error">{formError}</div>}

            <div className="form-row">
              <label htmlFor="lesson-title">Название урока</label>
              <input
                id="lesson-title"
                value={form.title}
                onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, title: e.target.value })}
                placeholder="Например: Знакомство на коми языке"
              />
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-2">
              <div className="form-row">
                <label htmlFor="lesson-order">Порядковый номер</label>
                <input
                  id="lesson-order"
                  type="number"
                  min={0}
                  value={form.order_index}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setForm({
                      ...form,
                      order_index: parseInt(e.target.value, 10) || 0,
                    })
                  }
                />
              </div>

              <div className="form-row">
                <label htmlFor="lesson-score">Проходной балл (%)</label>
                <input
                  id="lesson-score"
                  type="number"
                  min={0}
                  max={100}
                  value={form.passing_score}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setForm({
                      ...form,
                      passing_score: parseInt(e.target.value, 10) || 0,
                    })
                  }
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="form-row">
              <label htmlFor="lesson-attempts">
                Максимум попыток (пусто = без ограничений)
              </label>
              <input
                id="lesson-attempts"
                type="number"
                min={1}
                value={form.max_attempts ?? ""}
                onChange={(e) =>
                  setForm({
                    ...form,
                    max_attempts: e.target.value
                      ? parseInt(e.target.value, 10)
                      : null,
                  })
                }
                placeholder="Без ограничений"
              />
            </div>

            <div className="form-row">
              <label htmlFor="lesson-theory">Теоретический материал</label>
              <textarea
                id="lesson-theory"
                value={form.theory_content ?? ""}
                onChange={(e) =>
                  setForm({ ...form, theory_content: e.target.value })
                }
                placeholder="Текст теории урока..."
                rows={8}
              />
            </div>

            <div className="modal-footer">
              <button
                type="button"
                className="btn-secondary"
                onClick={closeForm}
                disabled={submitting}
              >
                Отмена
              </button>
              <button type="button" onClick={handleSubmitLesson} disabled={submitting}>
                {submitting
                  ? "Сохранение..."
                  : editingLessonId
                  ? "Сохранить изменения"
                  : "Создать урок"}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
````

````text
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import type { FormEvent } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";

import { api } from "../api/client";
import type { ModulePage } from "../types";
import { useAuth } from "../auth";

export default function ModulesPage() {
  const { user } = useAuth();

  const [data, setData] = useState<ModulePage | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [appliedSearch, setAppliedSearch] = useState("");

  const [title, setTitle] = useState("");
  const [description, setDescription] = useState("");
  const [level, setLevel] = useState("beginner");

  const canCreate = user?.role === "teacher" || user?.role === "admin";

  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    setError("");

    try {
      const params = new URLSearchParams({
        page: String(page),
        page_size: "10",
      });

      if (appliedSearch) {
        params.set("search", appliedSearch);
      }

      const response = await api.get<ModulePage>(
        `/modules?${params.toString()}`
      );

      setData(response);
    } catch (e) {
      setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Не удалось загрузить модули");
    }
  }, [page, appliedSearch]);

  useEffect(() => {
    load();
  }, [load]);

  const applySearch = () => {
    setPage(1);
    setAppliedSearch(search.trim());
  };

  const createModule = async (event: FormEvent) => {
    event.preventDefault();

    if (!title.trim()) {
      setError("Название модуля не может быть пустым");
      return;
    }

    try {
      await api.post("/modules", {
        title: title.trim(),
        description: description.trim() || null,
        level,
      });

      setTitle("");
      setDescription("");
      setLevel("beginner");
      setPage(1);

      await load();
    } catch (e) {
      setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Не удалось создать модуль");
    }
  };

  const totalPages = data
    ? Math.max(1, Math.ceil(data.total / data.page_size))
    : 1;

  return (
    <div className="container">
      <h1>Учебные модули</h1>

      {error && <div className="card error">{error}</div>}

      <div className="card toolbar">
        <input
          value={search}
          onChange={(event) => setSearch(event.target.value)}
          placeholder="Поиск по названию модуля"
          onKeyDown={(event) => {
            if (event.key === "Enter") {
              applySearch();
            }
          }}
        />
        <button type="button" onClick={applySearch}>
          Найти
        </button>
      </div>

      {canCreate && (
        <form className="card" onSubmit={createModule}>
          <h2>Создание модуля</h2>

          <div className="grid grid-2">
            <div className="form-row">
              <label htmlFor="title">Название</label>
              <input
                id="title"
                value={title}
                onChange={(event) => setTitle(event.target.value)}
                placeholder="Например: Знакомство на коми языке"
              />
            </div>

            <div className="form-row">
              <label htmlFor="level">Уровень</label>
              <select
                id="level"
                value={level}
                onChange={(event) => setLevel(event.target.value)}
              >
                <option value="beginner">beginner</option>
                <option value="intermediate">intermediate</option>
                <option value="advanced">advanced</option>
              </select>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="form-row">
            <label htmlFor="description">Описание</label>
            <textarea
              id="description"
              value={description}
              onChange={(event) => setDescription(event.target.value)}
              placeholder="Краткое описание учебного модуля"
            />
          </div>

          <button type="submit">Создать черновик</button>
        </form>
      )}

      <div className="card">
        {!data ? (
          <p>Загрузка...</p>
        ) : (
          <>
            <table>
              <thead>
                <tr>
                  <th>ID</th>
                  <th>Название</th>
                  <th>Уровень</th>
                  <th>Статус</th>
                  <th>Создан</th>
                  <th>Действия</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {data.items.length === 0 ? (
                  <tr>
                    <td colSpan={6}>Модули пока не найдены.</td>
                  </tr>
                ) : (
                  data.items.map((module) => (
                    <tr key={module.id}>
                      <td>{module.id}</td>
                      <td>
                        <Link
                          to={`/modules/${module.id}`}
                          className="module-link"
                        >
                          {module.title}
                        </Link>
                      </td>
                      <td>{module.level}</td>
                      <td>
                        <span
                          className={`badge badge-status-${module.status}`}
                        >
                          {module.status}
                        </span>
                      </td>
                      <td>
                        {new Date(module.created_at).toLocaleString("ru-RU")}
                      </td>
                      <td>
                        <Link
                          to={`/modules/${module.id}`}
                          className="btn-small"
                        >
                          Открыть
                        </Link>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  ))
                )}
              </tbody>
            </table>

            <div className="pagination">
              <button
                type="button"
                disabled={page <= 1}
                onClick={() => setPage((prev) => prev - 1)}
              >
                Назад
              </button>

              <span>
                Страница {data.page} из {totalPages}
              </span>

              <button
                type="button"
                disabled={page >= totalPages}
                onClick={() => setPage((prev) => prev + 1)}
              >
                Вперёд
              </button>
            </div>
          </>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
````

````text
import { useAuth } from "../auth";

export default function ProfilePage() {
  const { user, loading } = useAuth();

  if (loading) {
    return <div className="container">Загрузка...</div>;
  }

  if (!user) {
    return (
      <div className="container">
        <div className="card">
          <p>Пользователь не авторизован.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="container">
      <div className="card">
        <h1>Профиль</h1>

        <p>
          <strong>ID:</strong> {user.id}
        </p>

        <p>
          <strong>Email:</strong> {user.email}
        </p>

        <p>
          <strong>Имя:</strong> {user.full_name || "Не указано"}
        </p>

        <p>
          <strong>Роль:</strong> {user.role}
        </p>

        <p>
          <strong>Аккаунт активен:</strong>{" "}
          {user.is_active ? "Да" : "Нет"}
        </p>

        <p>
          <strong>Дата регистрации:</strong>{" "}
          {new Date(user.created_at).toLocaleString("ru-RU")}
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
````

````text
  const [data, setData] = useState<LessonProgressPage | null>(null);

  }, [page]);

  useEffect(() => {

  }, [load]);

  const totalPages = data
    ? Math.max(1, Math.ceil(data.total / data.page_size))
    : 1;

  return (
````

### Файл: frontend/src/pages/RegisterPage.tsx


````text
import { useState } from "react";
import { useForm } from "react-hook-form";
import { zodResolver } from "@hookform/resolvers/zod";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { z } from "zod";

import { useAuth } from "../auth";

const schema = z.object({
  email: z.string().email("Укажи корректный email"),
  fullName: z.string().max(255, "Слишком длинное имя").optional(),
  password: z.string().min(8, "Пароль должен содержать минимум 8 символов")
});

type FormData = z.infer<typeof schema>;

export default function RegisterPage() {
  const { register: registerUser } = useAuth();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const [serverError, setServerError] = useState("");

  const {
    register,
    handleSubmit,
    formState: { errors, isSubmitting }
  } = useForm<FormData>({
    resolver: zodResolver(schema),
    defaultValues: {
      email: "",
      fullName: "",
      password: ""
    }
  });

  const onSubmit = async (data: FormData) => {
    setServerError("");

    try {
      await registerUser(
        data.email,
        data.password,
        data.fullName ?? ""
      );

      navigate("/modules");
    } catch (error) {
      setServerError(
        error instanceof Error
          ? error.message
          : "Не удалось зарегистрироваться"
      );
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="container">
      <div className="card auth-card">
        <h1>Регистрация</h1>

        {serverError && <p className="error">{serverError}</p>}

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)}>
          <div className="form-row">
            <label htmlFor="email">Email</label>
            <input id="email" type="email" {...register("email")} />
            {errors.email && (
              <span className="error">{errors.email.message}</span>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="form-row">
            <label htmlFor="fullName">ФИО или имя</label>
            <input id="fullName" type="text" {...register("fullName")} />
            {errors.fullName && (
              <span className="error">{errors.fullName.message}</span>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="form-row">
            <label htmlFor="password">Пароль</label>
            <input id="password" type="password" {...register("password")} />
            {errors.password && (
              <span className="error">{errors.password.message}</span>
            )}
          </div>

          <button type="submit" disabled={isSubmitting}>
            {isSubmitting ? "Создание аккаунта..." : "Зарегистрироваться"}
          </button>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
````
